| `cs2-fokus-nemi1-2026-09-06` ($2,862 + $344) | Nemiga / FOKUS | `aec-cs2-fokus-nemi-2026-09-06` | nemiga BUY_SHORT / fokus BUY_LONG | premap |
| `cs2-k271-sin2-2026-09-06` ($1,489) | K27 | `aec-cs2-k27-sin-2026-09-06` | k27 BUY_LONG | premap |
| `cs2-rustec-for10-2026-09-07` ($2,301) | ex-RUSTEC | `aec-cs2-erx-for-2026-09-07` | ex rustec BUY_LONG | premap |
| `cs2-fokus-ast-2026-09-06` ($2,429) | ASTRAL | `aec-cs2-fokus-astr-2026-09-06` | astral BUY_SHORT | premap |

## 19. E2 (2026-09-07) — the map-winner family (`map_winner`): his `-game<N>` maps to the venue's per-team map row, subject side only

Read 13:50Z / 14:03Z (`nf-venue_1350.log` lines 311–318, `esports_chi_rows_1403.log` lines 280–326, verbatim). His `cs2-<a>-<b>-<date>-game<N>` with title `Counter-Strike: <A> vs <B> - Map <N> Winner` and a TEAM as his outcome read `unknown_market_type:unparsed` (`game1` is neither alpha, a line nor a team code) — $24.5k + $5.2k in 30 h. The venue lists ONE per-team yes/no row per map: `astatc-cs2-<a>-<b>-<date>-map<N>`, sides yes BUY_LONG / no BUY_SHORT, line `''`, question `Will <X> win Map <N> vs <Y>?` — `fnc-nip` map1/map2 name NIP, `hero-1win` map2 Heroic (no map1 listed at 13:50Z), `1win-astr` map2 1WIN, `fokus-nemi` map1/map2 FOKUS, `k27-sin` map2 K27, `k27-npg` map2, `sin-pha` map1/map2 Sinners.

**The readers.** `copy_sports.family_of` reads a lone `game<N>` as family `map_winner`, `segment_of` as `map<N>`; `market_type_of` stays `unknown` (the copy lane never reads it, as before); `mirror_family_of` files it `map_winner` and `MIRROR_FAMILIES` carries it. `premap.map_his` is the slug reader ({lg, a, b, date, n}); `_want_prefixes` answers {astatc} for it inside the identity switch.

**The rule (`premap._c3_pick_map`, matched_by `premap_map_winner`, source `premap`, behind `PREMAP_YN_IDENTITY` with C3; the wording arm never sees its rows).** In order: his title must fullmatch `Counter-Strike: <A> vs <B> - Map <N> Winner` with N equal to his slug's (`map:title-shear`); among the rows his keys fetched (his event title's matchup, §16 — so his codes need not equal the venue's: `1win-ast` reaches `1win-astr`), an `astatc-` identifier on his date whose suffix is exactly `map<N>` (`map:segment-absent` when the venue lists no map N on the event — `hero-1win -game1`); its question fullmatches `^will (?P<x>.+?) win map (?P<n>\d+) vs (?P<y>.+?)\??$` with n == N (`map:question-shape`); his outcome is exactly one side of his own title's matchup and `<x>` `_yn_name_match`es it while `<y>` matches the other side (`map:names`); his BUY of X is the row's `yes` side with the venue's own intent (`map:side` when no yes side is listed, `map:intent` when its intent is not BUY_LONG); two identifiers passing is `map:ambiguous`. His fill on Y — the venue lists no row with Y as its subject — refuses `map:subject-absent`: `no` on `NIP wins Map 1` is `fnatic wins Map 1` only if a listed map is never voided, which no row states, so the complement is NOT assumed (admit it only after a settled `no` row on a lost map is read). The same fact bears on the subject `yes` row itself: a voided map (a 2-0 series never plays map 3) settles the venue row and his market by rules no row states — accepted here as for the other derivatives (decision 17's ledger), said rather than assumed. **The event witness (review HIGH-1 / MEDIUM-1):** the matchup keys reach every astatc row titled `<A> vs. <B>` on his date whatever game code or team codes its identifier carries (the pair fields rosters in several games; a bracket's same-day rematch lists them under reversed codes), so the identifier's league code must equal his (the venue's esports codes read `cs2` on every row), and when ANY row on his date carries his own stem `astatc-<lg>-<a>-<b>-<date>-` (his codes byte for byte) an identifier outside it is another event — refused `map:segment-absent`, never a candidate, never an ambiguity; the digit-suffixed case (`1win-ast`) carries no stem on the board and is unchanged. Never a position, never a code similarity, never a nickname; the scope screen (`_yn_slot_bad`) is not applied to the two names — `_yn_name_match`'s token-set equality already refuses a qualifier on either side. `resolve_explain` carries `family`, `map` (the trace) and the refusal as the step's `split`, so MIRRORUNMAP prints `no_side_match:map:subject-absent`; it is set last so a C5 trace on a digit-suffixed code never relabels a family C5 has no lane for.

The venue lists `tsc-cs2-<a>-<b>-<date>-tot-2pt5` in the full C3 total grammar (over BUY_LONG / under BUY_SHORT, line 2.5, `Will the total in FOKUS vs. Nemiga be more than 2.5?`). `family_of` now reads it as the `total` family on the segment `maps` (`c3_his` line 2.5, seg `maps`), which no venue identifier carries as a segment token, so `c3_same_segment` empties the pool and the pick refuses `total:segment-absent` by name — a C3 total step, never the spread lane, never the wording arm. `market_type_of` types it `prop` (no venue prefix: with the switch off it refuses `unknown_market_type:family_not_listed`), exactly as a first-half total is — NOT `total`, because a `total` typing would let the wording arm map it onto that tsc row on line equality alone with the switch OFF, and the env may only lower rails. A `tot` that IS one of his slug's own team codes (a Tottenham pick side, its compact spread) is that code as before. The total-maps lane, the map handicap (`asc-…-hcap-(neg|pos)-<L>`, mostly still `1 50` sides in the 1403 read, sin-pha yes/no), the map-N round handicap and total rounds are NOT built (§3.5 of the gap note): questions read but no rule written.

**Left out, by design.** The map winner's other side (~$7k read); `sin2-cs1` (listing unread); the `1WIN`-first series phantom line (§16, the decision arm); an orientation rule for the complement until a settled map row is read. Every C1–C5 pin is green with the patch (`python -m pytest -q -x -p no:cacheprovider tests`).

The venue lists exact scores UNDER THE MONEYLINE PREFIX (render-ops `epl-rows`, `scratchpad/hard2/epl_rows_1338.log:241-314`; `premap_rows_1909.log:62-97` for La Liga; the investigation in `gap_new_families.md` §1.1, §2, §3.1): ~$86k of his book in 24 h (epl 50.8k, lal 18.7k, sea 9.1k, fl1 4.5k, bun 2.9k) died at `unknown_market_type:family_not_listed`.

| `…-exact-score-1-2` / `-3-0` / `-1-1` / `-0-0` | `…-exact-score-1-2` "Will EVE vs MNU finish MNU wins 2-1?" (:295); `-0-3` "… MNU wins 3-0?"; `-1-1` "… Draw 1-1?" (:293); `-other` "… finish Other?" (:313) — 17 identifiers a game, 34 rows of both EPL games read so |

### 20.1 The rule (`premap.c6_his`, `premap.c6_pick`, `premap._c6_pick_exact`; matched_by `premap_exact_score`, source `premap`)

1. **Trigger.** `family_of(slug) == 'exact_score'` with a full-game post-date rest of exactly `exact-score-X-Y` (digits); `_want_prefixes` answers `{atc}` (switch on). The older `-es-X-Y` form, a segmented exact score (`first-half-exact-score-…`) or any other tail: `exact:slug-shape`; `-exact-score-other`: `exact:other` ('Other' is every score the venue does not list — not a proposition his title states). Outcome Yes/No (`exact:outcome`). His title must fullmatch, after `fold_latin` and lower case, `^exact score: (?P<A>.+?) (?P<X>\d+) - (?P<Y>\d+) (?P<B>.+?)\??$` with A and B past the club scope screen (`exact:title-shape`) and X, Y equal to the slug's (`exact:title-score`).
2. **The row.** Among the atc- rows on his codes and date whose rest begins `exact-score-` (never the `-fh-exact-score-` twin, never a full-game moneyline row), his own league code when any row carries it, else the ONE other code (the C2 alias, `league_alias`; two: `exact:league-ambiguous`); none at all: `exact:family-absent` (today's name — step `unknown_market_type`, split `family_not_listed`, so the census bucket is unchanged); the family listed but not this score: `exact:no-row`. The identifier is then `atc-<code>-<a>-<b>-<date>-exact-score-X-Y` byte for byte — his slug under `atc-` (the C5 code translation rewrites `mun→mnu` / `che→cfc` first, as for every family: `c5_resolve` step 3 runs `c6_pick` on the rewritten slug). The row on his side: `side_norm` == his Yes/No, `intent` the venue's own for it (`exact:side`, `exact:ambiguous`, `exact:intent`). NEVER `_yn_line_ok`: every exact-score row in the dump carries a phantom line (`1` out of "wins 2-1", `0` out of "3-0"; `_question_line`), and `_market_rows` now stamps NO line on a `-exact-score-` identifier so the phantom reaches no other arm (the stored rows keep theirs until re-swept; the lane never reads it — pinned on rows carrying it).
3. **The question certifies the digit order.** Fullmatch `^will (?P<va>[a-z0-9]+) vs (?P<vb>[a-z0-9]+) finish (?:(?P<w>[a-z0-9]+) wins (?P<m>\d+)-(?P<n>\d+)|draw (?P<d1>\d+)-(?P<d2>\d+)|other)\??$` (`exact:question-shape`), `va`/`vb` the identifier's codes in its order (`exact:codes`); then X>Y ⇒ `w == va` and (m, n) == (X, Y); X<Y ⇒ `w == vb` and (m, n) == (Y, X); X==Y ⇒ `draw` with d1 == d2 == X; `other` on a scored identifier ⇒ `exact:other`; anything else `exact:order-uncertified` (the row's question in the trace). Never assumed from the identifier.
4. **The club witness.** His title binds X to his FIRST club A; A is the venue's `va` only through the event's own P4 rows: `atc-<code>-<a>-<b>-<date>-<a>` and `-<b>` must both be the venue's per-team contract (`_c5_sided`) whose question is the measured template on his date (`_yn_team_question`) with subject `_yn_name_match`-ing A and B respectively (`exact:club-unwitnessed`, `why`: `p4-row-absent:<code>`, `subject:<code>`, `yn:shape:<code>` …). A title with the clubs swapped ("Exact Score: Manchester United FC 2 - 1 Everton FC?" on `-2-1`) refuses here.

Like a segmented C3 pick, an exact score is read by the family's pick alone: the wording arm never sees the atc- rows the prefix admits (the wrong-market trap `_yn_title_is_his_game` names). Behind `PREMAP_YN_IDENTITY` with C2–C5: off, `_want_prefixes` carries no prefix and the family reads `unknown_market_type:family_not_listed` / `exact:family-absent` byte for byte as before. `resolve` returns the hit with `matched_by: premap_exact_score` (and `code_translated` / `witness` under C5, `league_alias` under the alias); `resolve_explain` carries the lane's trace as `c6` (or inside `yn_c5.lane` when C5 translated), and its refusal as the step's `split` (`no_side_match:exact:order-uncertified` …). `mirror_family_of` already files the slug `exact_score` (in `MIRROR_FAMILIES`).

The stored `markets.event_title` suffix (" - Exact Score") is the key grammar M1 builds (`event_keys_for`, `_yn_event_sides`), not this lane: the fixture keys on the matchup title as the C5 fixture did.

## 21. C6-HT (2026-09-07) — the halftime result by identity

The same gap one family over (`gap_new_families.md` §1.4, §3.3): ~$15k/24 h ($48k/30 h: bra 8.6k — NOT listed, 6 atc rows a game — fl1 7.2k, epl 5.0k, ere 3.5k, sea 2.2k) read `halftime:family-absent` while the venue lists `atc-<lg>-<a>-<b>-<date>-fh-<t>`:

| his feed (nf_his_1350.log) | the venue (nf_venue_1350.log:273-278, 297-300; premap_rows_1909.log:98-101) |
|---|---|
| `epl-ars-che-2026-09-06-halftime-result-away` — "Chelsea FC leading at halftime?" No/Yes ($2.4k); `-home` "Arsenal FC leading at halftime?" | `atc-epl-ars-cfc-2026-09-06-fh-cfc` yes LONG / no SHORT, line empty — "Will Chelsea FC lead Arsenal FC at halftime?"; `-fh-ars` "Will Arsenal FC lead Chelsea FC at halftime?" |
| `fl1-olm-pfc-…-halftime-result-draw` — "Olympique de Marseille vs. Paris FC: Draw at halftime?" ($6.3k) | `-fh-draw` "Will ARS vs CFC be tied at halftime?"; `atc-lal-esp-sev-…-fh-draw` "Will ESP vs SEV be tied at halftime?"; `-fh-esp` "Will RCD Espanyol de Barcelona lead Sevilla FC at halftime?" |
| `epl-eve-mun-…-halftime-result-away` "Manchester United FC leading at halftime?" | `atc-epl-eve-mnu-…-fh-eve` "Will Everton FC lead Manchester United FC at halftime?" (the `-fh-mnu` row was cut by the preset's LIMIT); `-sh-mnu` "Will Manchester United FC win the second half against Everton FC?" — never a candidate |

### 21.1 The rule (`premap._c6_pick_halftime`; matched_by `premap_halftime`, source `premap`)

1. **Trigger.** `family_of(slug) == 'halftime_result'`: `…-halftime-result-(home|away|draw)` (`halftime:slug-shape` otherwise), outcome Yes/No (`halftime:outcome`), prefix `{atc}`. Candidates are the atc- rows on his codes and date whose rest is exactly `fh-<t>`, t ∈ {a, b, draw} — never `-sh-`, never `-fh-exact-score-`, never a full-game row; league as §20.1 step 2 (`halftime:league-ambiguous`; none: `halftime:family-absent` under today's step/split; no per-team `-fh-` row for a home/away pick: `halftime:no-row`).
2. **Draw.** His title is the matchup and the qualifier `'<A> vs. <B>: Draw at halftime?'` (`_c3_title_parts`, qualifier exactly `draw at halftime`; else `halftime:title-shape`); the `-fh-draw` row on his side (`halftime:no-row` / `halftime:side` / `halftime:ambiguous` / `halftime:intent`); its question fullmatches `^will (?P<va>[a-z0-9]+) vs (?P<vb>[a-z0-9]+) be tied at halftime\??$` (`halftime:question-shape`) with the identifier's codes (`halftime:codes`); the P4 rows bind A/B to a/b as §15.1 step 4 (`halftime:subject-unwitnessed`, `why`).
3. **Home / away — NEVER a position.** His title fullmatches `^(?P<S>.+?) leading at halftime\??$` naming ONE club S past the club scope screen (`halftime:title-shape`); his other club O is the side of his event title that is not S (`_yn_event_sides`; none: `halftime:event-unwitnessed`; S not a side of it: `halftime:event-shear`). Among the `-fh-<a>` / `-fh-<b>` rows, the one whose question fullmatches `^will (?P<s>.+?) lead (?P<o>.+?) at halftime\??$` with `_yn_name_match(s, S)` and `_yn_name_match(o, O)` is his — none parses: `halftime:question-shape`; none names S: `halftime:subject-unwitnessed` (the subjects seen in the trace); two: `halftime:ambiguous`. The side row he is placed on must carry the same question (review LOW-1: `why: side-row-question`). The code t is then read from THAT row's identifier (`fh_code` in the trace) and HELD AGAINST THE CLUB (review MEDIUM-1): the event's P4 row `-<t>` must be the venue's per-team contract whose subject is S, and the other P4 row's subject O (`_c6_clubs_witnessed`; else `halftime:subject-unwitnessed` with `why: code-shear:<t>` and the P4 reason in `p4`) — a venue event whose two `-fh-` questions are swapped against their identifiers (the shape `c5_certify` names `rows-swapped`) refuses instead of trading the question. His slug's `home` / `away` token is never read — his `-home` slug with a title naming Chelsea lands on `-fh-cfc`.

Behind `PREMAP_YN_IDENTITY`; C5 translates the codes first (`che→cfc`, witnessed by the event's P4 rows and his event title); the C2 alias carries the league (`fl1->lg1`). The mirror files the slug under `market_type_of`'s `prop` (`mirror_family_of` unchanged), which `MIRROR_FAMILIES` admits.

| `epl-eve-mun-…-halftime-result-away` "Manchester United FC leading at halftime?" with `-fh-eve` and `-sh-mnu` listed | | | | `no_side_match:halftime:subject-unwitnessed` — `-sh-` never a candidate; no `-fh-` per-team row: `halftime:no-row` |
| `-fh-draw` worded "Will ESP vs SEV be tied …" on the lg1 identifier | | | | `no_side_match:halftime:codes` |
| a P4 row missing / a title naming another club / a qualified title | | | | `halftime:subject-unwitnessed` / `halftime:title-shape` |
| `sea-juv-mil-…-halftime-result-draw` on an event listing no `-fh-` row; switch off | | | | `unknown_market_type:family_not_listed`, `halftime:family-absent` |

Left out: bra / ven1 / bol1 halftime rows (not read; bra inferred absent); the second-half result (`-sh-`, no feed row); a title with no event title stored (`no_key_intersection` before the lane, and `halftime:event-unwitnessed` inside it); the ' - Halftime Result' event-title suffix (M1's key grammar).

## 22. W2 (2026-09-07) — the live lane names every candidate refusal, reads the long token when his fills sit on the short-side token, and rotates the candidate walk

Investigation `gap_planned_unopened.md` (mirror_live.py at 1c1e1d7 / f1f2fb2): 58 mapped tennis and football markets ($218k in 24 h) had a shadow plan on a two-sided OPEN book while he traded and NEVER had a mirror_books row, and the lane's own verdict on each survived nowhere — the census counts a name once per tick, the heartbeat keeps the last tick's ~20 `recent` events, and four exits of `_tick_candidate` returned silently. Three changes, built on W1 (the same files); one live change on the candidate path, no order path touched.

- P1 `long_token_unknown` (mirror_live `_tick_candidate`, after the grammar certification and before the book check and the quote read) — class B, $26.9k proven and up to $79k: every fill of his sits on the venue's SHORT-side token (the aec tennis family's `<his side>:SHORT`), so `_choose_long` named the long side `other_of(a)` over HIS tokens and found none, the mapping came back `long_asset: None`, and `if not la: return` left silently every tick until he bought the other token (14 of 22 mapped markets on 2026-09-07 opened that way, 15-127 min each). The lane now reads the long token off the catalogue exactly as it reads the OTHER token for a long-only fill — the condition's sibling of the token in hand, `_SQL_SIBLING_TOKEN` (one text for both lanes since W2, `ms.SIBLING_TOKEN_SQL`; identity only, never a name or a position guess) — and the EXISTING short road runs unchanged from there: `rules.mirror_target`'s signed target, `_his_level(reducing=True, short=True)` = his other-token BUY at round(1 − p, 6), BUY_SHORT behind `short_model_confirmed` / `_short_gate` / S4. A catalogue that does not name it (absent, unreadable, or the mapper naming neither token) refuses `long_token_unknown` and memoises the market for `UNMAPPED_TTL_S` (900 s): no venue read, no book, never a token the catalogue does not name. The gates are not widened: the same fills are refused `short_side_refused` / `short_model_disarmed` / `short_gate_refused` exactly as a short whose long token the mapper named, with the same row. The shadow's row agrees: `ms.map_market` fills a `long_asset: None` mapping from the same catalogue row (`_long_from_catalogue`, `detail.long_from = catalogue`), so `long_asset` is set on the mapped row and `his_long` reads 0 against `his_other`; a catalogue that names nothing leaves the row's `long_asset` NULL and marks `detail.long_token_unknown` (the shadow measures; it still plans as before). Pinned: `test_w2_fills_only_on_the_short_side_token_open_a_short_book_through_the_existing_gates`, `test_w2_a_short_side_only_candidate_is_refused_by_the_gates_exactly_as_one_whose_long_token_was_named`, `test_w2_an_unnamed_long_token_refuses_long_token_unknown_before_any_read`, `test_w2_the_long_path_is_unchanged_when_the_mapper_names_the_long_token`; on the shadow `test_w2_the_mapper_fills_the_long_token_from_the_catalogue_when_his_fills_sit_on_the_short_side`, `test_w2_the_shadow_row_carries_the_catalogue_long_token_and_names_an_unnamed_one`.
- P2 every exit of `_tick_candidate` returns a NAME (the census counters `_mirror_stop` already had are unchanged); the four silent ones are `long_token_unknown`, `target_zero` (ratio × net rounding to nothing), `book_row_unreadable` (the book opened and its row could not be read back; it is walked from its row next tick), and `cand_unread_capped` (the walk's cap — the candidates' budget or the soft guard — left the candidate unread this tick; never one a memo or a book would have skipped anyway). `book_seen` (a market with a book) is the one name counted and written nowhere. One row per (whale, condition_id) TRANSITION lands on `mirror_candidate_refusals` (migration 054: whale, condition_id, us_slug, refusal, at, his_net, target, mark, his_px, ask, band, long_asset, books_live, opened_today, active_conditions, cand_reads, tick_s): a row when the name differs from the last one written for the pair, and again when the same name has stood for `CAND_REFUSAL_RESTAMP_S` (900 s, the `_unmapped_until` memo class), so the writes are bounded by his active conditions over 900 s; a book opening writes no row and sets the pair's memo to `opened`, so a refusal after the book closes is a transition again. The row carries only what the tick already held when the candidate left (NULL for what it never reached: "not reached", never 0), and no venue call is made for it. The tick's rows go out as ONE statement after the walk (`_SQL_CAND_REFUSALS`, `jsonb_to_recordset`); a write failure never blocks the tick — `refusal_write_failed` on the census every tick it fails, logged once per process, and the memo never takes the failed write, so the row lands on the next tick that can write it (the table is absent until 054 is applied; the workers never run migrations). Census keys added before the pinned last key: `long_token_unknown`, `target_zero`, `book_row_unreadable`, `cand_unread_capped`, `refusal_write_failed` (past the served prefix; `integ` is at its ceiling). Render-ops: the read-only `cand-refusals` preset prints the last 24 h by refusal with his dollars joined (the latest refusal per market and its no-book part; every name's rows with the tick's wall time, candidate reads and his active conditions; the 40 largest no-book markets with their refusal path through the day), and `coverage-gap` prefers this table's refusal (`live:<name>`, the newest row at or before his last fill, else the newest) over the shadow's verdict for a market with no book. Pinned: `test_w2_every_exit_of_the_candidate_returns_a_name_and_every_name_is_a_census_key`, `test_w2_one_row_per_transition_and_a_restamp_after_900_s`, `test_w2_a_refusal_write_failure_never_blocks_the_tick_and_is_logged_once`, `test_w2_a_book_row_that_cannot_be_read_back_is_named_and_the_book_is_walked_next_tick`, `test_w2_target_zero_is_named`, `test_w2_the_walks_cap_names_every_candidate_it_left_unread`; the 054 pins in `test_mirror_live_migration.py`.
- P3 the candidate walk rotates (`_candidate_order`, `_cand_cursor`, `_shadow_planned`): the woken markets first as before, then the candidates whose NEWEST shadow row carries a plan (a `would_side`, or a target that is not 0; one `mirror_shadow` read per whale per tick, `_SQL_SHADOW_PLANNED`; unreadable = no plan known, never a stop) ahead of the rest, each group newest-fill-first; a tick that broke on the cap resumes AFTER the last candidate it walked next tick, so every readable candidate is walked within ceil(readable / `MAX_MARKETS_PER_TICK`) ticks (100 candidates at the cap of 40: three ticks); a walk that reached the end of its list clears the cursor, so an uncapped tick keeps the order it always had. The cap is unchanged (`MIRROR_LIVE_MAX_MARKETS`, env may only lower it) and the memos (`_unmapped_until`, `_terminal_until`, the full-game memo, a market with a book) still skip BEFORE any read, spending no slot. Pinned: `test_w2_the_candidate_walk_rotates_planned_first_woken_first_and_the_memos_still_skip`.
- Reading the census after W2: `cand_unread_capped` on the heartbeat is how many candidates the cap left unread (0 on an uncapped tick); `refusal_write_failed` rising every tick means 054 is not applied (or the write is failing) and the rows are being retried; `long_token_unknown` is a mapped market whose long token neither his fills nor the catalogue name — a catalogue gap to read, never a mapping to guess. Names kept as they are: `max_books` (lifted 5df3975), `side_band` (named on the row, the rule kept), `short_side_refused` (lifted), `mirror_loss_stop` (owner).

## 23. C6 (2026-09-07) — college football: the venue's own team field binds mascot, code and school

The bucket (`gap_cfb.md`, census 13:33Z): $283,124 of RN1's college football refused in 24 h — six moneylines ($143,593) at one gate (`grammar_echo_unverified`: the venue lists no plain `atc-cfb-…-<code>` contract, only `winner-<seg>-<code>` rows the admission skipped), the lou-miss / wisc-nd / washst-wash spreads ($37.8k + $34.1k) at C4's subject step (`spread:subject-uncertified`: no class record; `spread:code-unmatched` / `title-unreadable`: the two-letter `nd` no code rule reads; `spread:pair-unwitnessed`: his title naming his own team), and $58k of lines the venue does not list (unchanged). §10.1 had said "no stored venue row names a mascot AND its school together" — true of the TABLE, because the sweep dropped the field. The probe (engine-diagnostic run 34131932442, side-truth job, 14:15:54Z) printed it, verbatim:

```
aec-cfb-smu-flst-2026-09-07 side[0] description='Mustangs' team={"abbreviation": "smu", "alias": "Mustangs", "conference": "Atlantic Coast", "displayAbbreviation": "SMU", "id": 1067, "league": "cfb", "name": "Mustangs", "ordering": "away", "ranking": "19", "record": "0-0", "safeName": "SMU", ...} teamId=1067 long=True
aec-cfb-smu-flst-2026-09-07 side[1] description='Seminoles' team={"abbreviation": "flst", "alias": "Seminoles", "displayAbbreviation": "FSU", "id": 1090, "league": "cfb", "name": "Seminoles", "ordering": "home", "record": "1-0", "safeName": "Florida State", ...} teamId=1090 long=False
aec-cfb-lou-miss-2026-09-06 side[0] description='Cardinals' team={"abbreviation": "lou", "alias": "Cardinals", "displayAbbreviation": "LOU", "id": 1085, "league": "cfb", "name": "Cardinals", "ordering": "away", "ranking": "24", "record": "0-1", "safeName": "Louisville", ...} teamId=1085 long=True
aec-cfb-lou-miss-2026-09-06 side[1] description='Rebels' team={"abbreviation": "miss", "alias": "Rebels", "displayAbbreviation": "MISS", "id": 1258, "league": "cfb", "name": "Rebels", "ordering": "home", "ranking": "9", "record": "1-0", "safeName": "Ole Miss", ...} teamId=1258 long=False
atc-cfb-wisc-nd-2026-09-06-winner-1h-nd question='Will Notre Dame win the first half?' side[0] description='Yes' team=null; side[1] description='No' team=null
atc-cfb-lou-miss-2026-09-06-winner-1h-miss question='Will Ole Miss win the first half?' side[0] 'Yes' team=null; side[1] 'No' team=null
(market-level team=null on every market; eventSlug None on every market.)
```

On EVERY aec-cfb side the venue states, on the moneyline row itself, `abbreviation` EQUAL TO THE SLUG CODE, `safeName` the school, `name`/`alias` the mascot. The mascot↔code↔school binding is venue-stated; no positional assumption is needed anywhere. Identity only, in the C1–C5 discipline: token-set equality (`pmus._yn_name_match`), never a prefix; `name`/`alias` never read as the school; `ordering` never read; every disagreement a named refusal. EVERY C6 read — the spread chain (17.2–17.4) AND the moneyline class (17.5) — is behind `PREMAP_YN_IDENTITY` (`premap.yn_identity_on`, read at each of the three moneyline sites: M5 review HIGH-1); off, the mapper and the class are byte-identical to C5. The moneyline arm is confined to `lg == 'cfb'` for now (the soccer builder lands its own reader of the same columns; M5 review LOW-2). Tests: `backend/tests/test_c6_cfb_team.py` (the probe's dicts verbatim for lou-miss and smu-flst; the wisc-nd / scarst-flam dicts built in the probe's shape and marked so; the venue rows of `c4_rows_0305.log` / `cfb_venue_1353.log`) and the review's pins, `backend/tests/test_c6_cfb_review_pins.py`.

### 23.1 C6c-store — the sweep keeps the field (`premap._side_team`, `_market_rows`, `_upsert`; migration `055_us_premap_team.sql`)

Per side: `team_abbr` (abbreviation, lower), `team_name` (name, folded as the side descriptions are — the mascot), `team_safe_name` (safeName, folded — the school), `team_id` (teamId / team.id), `team_league` (team.league), and the market's `game_start` (gameStartTime) and `sports_type` (sportsMarketType); the UPSERT rewrites all seven on conflict. Nothing is derived in the sweep: an absent or unreadable field is NULL, and every reader takes NULL as "the venue stated nothing". The column names are shared with the soccer builder's patch of the same day (M6) so both merge (055: 054 is W2's). **The deploy window (M5 review MEDIUM-4).** The workers never migrate — the API's `start.sh` runs `migrate` before `uvicorn`, and the worker services deploy from the same commit beside it — so a worker can boot before 055 is applied. The sweep adds the seven columns itself in `_ensure_table` (`ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`, the `signed` precedent of 031), so the writer never waits on the migrate; the readers that run no sweep (the shadow, the live worker) PROBE for them (`premap.team_columns_present`: one `information_schema.columns` read per process, cached; a present answer is final, an absent or unreadable one is asked again every 600 s so a worker that booted first picks the columns up without a restart), and `resolve` / `resolve_explain` (`team_select_cols`) and `_upsert` use the pre-C6 statement while they are absent: nothing reads `premap_query_failed`, and every C6 reader refuses on the team-less rows exactly as on a row the venue stated nothing for.

### 23.2 C6a — the venue's code witness (`map_lane.code_school`, `winner_school`, `same_name`)

Over the event's `atc-<lg>-<a>-<b>-<date>-winner-<seg>-<code>` rows with code ∈ {a, b}: the question `^Will (?P<school>.+?) win the (first|second|third|fourth) (half|quarter)\?$` names the school; every parsed row per code must name the same school, both codes must be witnessed, the two schools must differ. `{lou: louisville, miss: ole miss}`, `{wisc: wisconsin, nd: notre dame}` on the verbatim rows; the `-draw` rows ("Will the first half end tied?") and prop rows state nothing. Refusals `spread:code-witness-disagree` (one code, two schools — `-nd` "Notre Dame" beside "North Dakota"), `spread:code-witness-same`. It replaces `code_reads` in `_c4_subject_by_code` ONLY where it exists: his outcome / his title's team take a position by `_yn_name_match(school, name)` (`spread:code-unnamed` when neither school is named — "Mississippi State" beside the witness, where C4's prefix read `miss` and refused `code-collision`; `spread:code-both`; `spread:title-unreadable` as before). Absent (scarst-flam, txs-pvam, wcar-camp, smu-flst: no atc rows) today's rules stand — `nd` stays unreadable exactly as C4 pinned.

### 23.3 C6b — the event-title pair witness (`premap._c6_pair_by_event`, `_c6_event_sides`; `c3_pick` gets `his_event_title`)

The named follow-up of §10.7: when his outcome == his title's team ("Ole Miss" / "Spread: Ole Miss (-1.5)"), his event title "Louisville vs. Ole Miss" must resolve to the two DIFFERENT positions (through the C6a witness, else `code_reads`) with his outcome's name on his. No event title (lou-miss 13.5/4.5/7.5, wisc-nd 10.5/24.5/2.5: `markets.event_title` NULL): `spread:pair-unwitnessed`, unchanged; a name reading no or both positions ("Baylor vs. Auburn" on this game): `spread:pair-unwitnessed`; two names on one position ("Ole Miss vs. Ole Miss"), or a second name of his on his own position ("Louisville vs. Mississippi State" for an Ole Miss fill): `spread:event-collision`. The reader splits on " vs " after the C3 fold and applies no free-slot screen ("Florida A&M" folds to `florida a m`, which the yes/no lane's screen refuses and this reader may not: the names are never a lookup key). Trace: `pair_via: event`, `event_hits`.

### 23.4 C6c — the subject from the venue's row (`premap._c6_team_subject`, inside `_c4_subject_certified`)

After C4's aec checks (the row present, its two sides exactly the question's names) and the class's rails (state readable, not tripped — kept): when BOTH aec sides carry `team_abbr`, the question's subject (names[0], slug a by the grammar) and its opponent are certified from the row — each side's abbreviation must be one of the slug codes (else `spread:subject-uncertified`, subject `team-unnamed`), its `team_safe_name` must name that code's school (the C6a witness's, else his own words for that position as the pair witness read them; else subject `team-school`), and its code's position must equal its slot in the question — the subject's mascot bound to b, or the opponent's to a, is the venue contradicting the grammar: `spread:subject-conflict`, nothing maps on the row. One side with a team and one without: subject `team-absent`. No team on either side: the class's record certifies as in C4 (subject `grammar-uncertified` with `team: absent` when there is none). A class record for the aec market that DISAGREES with the row's binding (Cardinals certified at position 1 beside a row binding Cardinals to `lou`) is two venue statements in time, a wrong side one of the two times — `spread:subject-conflict`, subject `record-team` (M5 review MEDIUM-3; an agreeing record, or one of another shape, changes nothing). Then C3's table unchanged; matched_by `premap_spread_code`, trace `subject: {certified, code, via: team}`.

### 23.5 C6-ML — the moneyline class (`map_lane._team_hits` in `aec_code_side`, `_contract_candidates`, `map_lane._team_truth` in `grammar_truth`, `_grammar_admission`)

Under the identity switch and on `cfb` only. Where no code rule reads his outcome (`nd` / "Notre Dame"), the aec side whose `team.abbreviation` is a slug code and whose `team.safeName` names his outcome puts him at that code's position — BOTH sides must state an abbreviation (half a binding is none, the bar the spread reader holds: M5 review MEDIUM-1); the index must still agree (a side stating a code at the OTHER index is the venue's order contradicting the slug's: `side_code_conflict`); both sides naming his school: `side_code_ambiguous`; a mascot-only or half-bound payload: `side_code_unmatched` as today. The shadow carries `his_outcome` on the grammar record. Live, `_contract_candidates` admits the `winner-<seg>-<code_i>` rows as the code's contract where the venue lists no plain one — suffix exactly `winner-<seg>-<code_i>`, never `-draw`, question naming the school; every such row must name ONE school and the first by name stands for them all (two schools: every row, so the admission reads "more than one" and refuses). `grammar_truth(…, code=, school=)` then reads the winner contract against the aec market's OWN head (`map_lane.aec_head`: `aec-<lg>-<a>-<b>-<date>`): the contract slug must EQUAL `atc-<lg>-<a>-<b>-<date>-winner-<seg>-<code_i>` (never a suffix read: another date or event is `unverified`, M5 review LOW-1), and the side at position i must state `abbreviation == code_i` AND `safeName` naming the contract's school (and his outcome, when carried), the other side stating a team and claiming neither → `ok`, the record `certified[aec] = {outcome_desc, side_index, his_slug}` is written and the book may open; any disagreement (the side's school, his outcome, the contract naming the other school, a name/alias-only team, the other side claiming the code or the school) → `mismatch`, `side_echo_mismatch`, the class trips; either side stating no team → `unverified`, nothing tripped. The plain contracts of C1/C4 read exactly as before, with one rail added under the switch (M5 review MEDIUM-2): a plain contract's name never overrides the side's own code — the side stating the OTHER slug code, or the other side stating this one, is `mismatch`; an abbreviation that is neither code is not evidence.

## 24. C7 (2026-09-07) — soccer: the game by the venue's kickoff instant, the club by the venue's team record

Owner 14:45Z: "the soccer mapping ... can be solved 100%". §17.3's last row is the gap: the venue DATES his Liga Portugal games 2026-09-05 where his slugs say 2026-09-06 and names the clubs otherwise, so no dated key of his meets the rows and the names refuse by design ($80k/24 h). The engine-diagnostic PREMAP-TEAM read (run 34134258854, side-truth job, 14:40:49Z) shows what the venue's market payload STATES, verbatim (trimmed to the fields that matter):

```

atc-lco-per-mil-2026-09-06-mil  gameStartTime "2026-09-06T23:10:00Z"  side[0] 'Yes' team={abbreviation "mil", alias "", name "Millonarios FC", id 17080, league "lco", providerIds [{SPORTSDATAIO 1201}], safeName ""} teamId 17080
atc-pdc-hua-col-2026-09-06-col  gameStartTime "2026-09-06T20:00:00Z"  side[0] 'Yes' team={abbreviation "col", name "CSD Colo-Colo", id 17150, league "pdc", safeName ""}
atc-srb-ver-are-2026-09-06-ver  gameStartTime "2026-09-06T15:15:00Z"  side[0] 'Yes' team={abbreviation "ver", name "Hellas Verona FC", id 26916, league "srb", safeName ""}
atc-epl-eve-mnu-2026-09-06-mnu  gameStartTime "2026-09-06T13:00:00Z"  side[0] 'Yes' team={abbreviation "mnu", alias "The Red Devils", name "Manchester United FC", id 2694, league "epl", providerIds [{SPORTSDATAIO 517},{SPORTRADAR sr:competitor:35}], record "1-1-1", safeName "Manchester United"}
tsc-epl-eve-mnu-2026-09-06-2pt5  gameStartTime "2026-09-06T13:00:00Z"  sportsMarketType "soccer_team_full_game_total"  sides Over/Under team=null; market keys also carry 'line', 'spreadTotalSuffix'
(market-level team=null everywhere; eventSlug None everywhere; tsc/draw rows carry team=null)
```

Every per-team soccer row carries, on BOTH its yes/no sides, a `team` record for the SUBJECT club — `abbreviation` equal to the identifier's code, `name` the club as the venue writes it, `id`/`teamId`, `league` — and every market carries `gameStartTime`, an INSTANT: 2026-09-06T19:30:00Z on the row whose SLUG says 2026-09-05. His side: the CLOB market record's `game_start_time` (`sportsassets/clob.py`), read per condition_id by `edge_marks._game_start` and cached in `market_starts` (migration 044). An instant is the game's identity where the venue's slug date is not; the venue's own two records bind code → club for the event.

### 24.1 The store (§23's `premap._side_team`, `_market_rows`, `_upsert`, `_ensure_table`; migration 055 — one store, no second column)

C7 reads the seven columns §23 stores per row: `team_abbr` (team.abbreviation, lower), `team_name` (team.name — the soccer CLUB: 'Millonarios FC' where safeName is ''; the mascot on college rows, which §23 never reads as the school), `team_league` (team.league), `game_start` (gameStartTime, tz-aware), `sports_type` (sportsMarketType); `team_safe_name` and `team_id` (bigint: the venue's numeric id, a non-numeric one stores nothing) are §23's. ONE fold rule: both names go through the explicit Latin fold the yes/no arms read club names with (`pmus.fold_latin`, then `_norm`: 'Tromsø' stores 'tromso', the venue's own ASCII; ASCII names unchanged). ONE parser (`_game_start`): an ISO instant with its offset ('Z') or an epoch number; a NAIVE time — a date alone, a clock without an offset — stores nothing (it would be keyed to a guessed zone). Nullable, unbackfilled: a row not yet swept reads NULL, which C7 refuses. Nothing is read from `ordering`, `alias` or `safeName`. ONE SELECT: §23's `TEAM_SELECT_COLS` carries C7's four names beside its two, through the same `information_schema` probe (`team_columns_present`), so a database without 055 never reads `premap_query_failed` and C7 refuses on the NULLs.

### 24.2 The key (`venue_kick_keys`, `his_kick_keys`; the sweep; `_dated_admissible`)

The sweep keys EVERY row of an event by `<team_name>@<ISO minute>` for each club the event's full-time-winner records name (both clubs, on the draw and the family rows too; the markets fallback keys the subject alone, one market at a time); his side keys his title's anchor, his event title's two sides, his draw title's and his family title's two sides at HIS instant (`his_kick_keys`). His instant: `_his_kick` — inside `PREMAP_YN_IDENTITY`, with a `condition_id`, for a `<lg>-<a>-<b>-<date>-<rest>` slug. THE LIVE PATH IS READ-ONLY (M6 review H3): `resolve` — the copy lane before every first fill, mirror_live's tick under its lock, the shadow's map — reads `market_starts` and nothing else; no row, a failed read or a record without a game start is 'unread' / 'no-game-start' → no kickoff key, `kick:unknown` downstream, never a guess. `resolve_explain(fetch_kick=True)` — the shadow's re-judge, which E2 paces — asks `edge_marks._game_start`, one bounded read of the public CLOB cached in `market_starts` (failures retried on the job's own clock), so the table fills within a tick and the next live resolve maps; the API census passes `fetch_kick=False` and never fans out venue reads. `resolve` / `resolve_explain` take `condition_id` (the shadow's `map_market` / `explain_unmapped` ctx, the live lane's payload and the census hand it). An "@" key, admitted as the title keys are: an instant is game agreement by construction. A lookup only, never a date-only key.

### 24.3 The rule (`premap._c7_kick_pick`, `_c7_kick_draw_pick`, `_c7_certify`, `_c7_events`; `kick_c3_resolve` for the families)

Inside `_yn_pick`, AFTER the alias arm and the names lane (§17) refused — on `yn:no-row`, `yn:names-mismatch`, `yn:names-unwitnessed`, `yn:names-ambiguous` only; a shear of his own words (`yn:event-shear`, `yn:names-shear`), his own identifier refused, an alias-ambiguous suffix or a gate refusal on a witnessed row stand — and standing aside where C5 owns the shape (`_c5_owns`). Then, ALL of:

1. **His instant known** (else `kick:unknown`); the venue's full-time-winner rows (`sports_type soccer_team_full_time_winner`, a team record on the side) whose `game_start` EQUALS his to the MINUTE — another minute is another game (`kick:no-event`; rows at the instant typed otherwise and none that is: `kick:type`).
2. **Exactly one event** among them (one `<lg>-<a>-<b>-<date>` stem with BOTH per-team rows, yes/no sided and unlined as C5 reads them — `_c5_sided`; a lined or intent-swapped market is `partial` and no witness — each P4 question passing the club and league-slot screens on its OWN identifier's date and naming the record's club) whose two `team_name`s contain at least ONE club equal to one of his event title's sides — his title's anchor alone when no event title is stored — under token-set equality (`pmus._yn_name_match`). Two: `kick:ambiguous` (`candidates`). None: `kick:club-unwitnessed` (`venue_names`). A women's twin ('Liga Portugal Feminina') is screened at the league slot and is no candidate (`screened`).
3. **The record agrees with the identifier**: `team_abbr` is the identifier's code at the row's position, `team_league` its league, the question's subject the record's club, the two sides of one market one club — else the venue contradicts itself: `kick:code-conflict`, map nothing. The venue's own two records bind code → club, so his OTHER club — the one whose name differs ('Gil Vicente FC' vs 'Gil Vicente Barcelos') — is bound BY EXCLUSION: a club plays one match at one instant, the venue lists two teams for it, one is proven, the other is his other side. THE EXCLUSION NEVER BINDS A CONTRADICTION (review M1): his stated name for the other club and the venue's record must share a token beyond legal-form furniture (`_C7_FURNITURE`: the pinned `GENERIC_CLUB_TOKENS` plus 'sd', 'ud') — 'Gil Vicente FC' / 'Gil Vicente Barcelos', 'CD Santa Clara' / 'Santa Clara Azores', 'Sporting CP' / 'Sporting Braga' share their name; 'Sporting CP' / 'Boavista FC' share nothing and refuse `kick:club-unwitnessed` why `names` (`his_club`, `venue_club`) — a data error in one feed, never traded. `club_by_exclusion {his: venue}` rides the hit (his club as his event title names it, or his slug's code when no event title is stored — nothing to veto then) so the census counts how often the exclusion carried a pick; `witness_src` says whether the picked row's own record (`record`) or the other row (`exclusion`) proved it.
4. **His title's subject is one of his two event-title sides, at his slug tail's position** (t == a → 0; the other side or a reversed event title: `kick:title-shear`, as §17 reads it), and the row picked is the venue row whose `team_abbr` is the code at that SAME position — that row's club must be his anchor, proven or by exclusion (`kick:club-unwitnessed`, why `position`: the venue ordering the pair otherwise, `acv-gil`) — never `home`/`away`, never `ordering`.
5. **`_yn_gate`**: the venue's own side and intent for his Yes/No; then `match_side`'s `_yn_line_ok` and identity veto as for every arm.

**The draw**: the event certified as above from his two sides (his event title, else his draw title; the draw title gate as `_yn_draw_pick` applies it), at their positions; then that event's `-draw` row — its own `game_start` at the instant — through `_yn_draw_row` on the VENUE's two names on the identifier's date. **The families**: once the stem is certified for his event (his event title's sides, else his `<A> vs. <B>: …` title's), his slug is rewritten to it and `c3_pick` runs on that stem's tsc / asc / astatc rows exactly as `resolve` runs it (`kick_c3_resolve`, after C5, `names_resolve`, the exact-score / halftime lane (§20-21) and the map lane (§19) — last of every arm; the family lane's own witness — names, line, segment, sign — still decides: the btts row naming 'Gil Vicente Barcelos' refuses `btts:names`); a family row stating another instant than its event refuses (`kick:no-event`, why `family-row`). A FULL-GAME total needs no ride: once the kickoff key fetched its row, the wording arm maps it as every full-game total (§11, matched_by `premap`) — BUT THE KICKOFF KEY IS A LOOKUP, NEVER A DECISION (review H1 / H2, `_c7_wording_guard`, both readers, the plain and the armed wording call): a wording hit, with his instant known, (a) stating another instant than his refuses `kick:no-event` why `family-row`; (b) of a FOREIGN stem (not his `<a>-<b>-<date>`: reachable by the instant alone) must state his instant itself (NULL refuses) and its stem must be the ONE event `_c7_certify` certifies from his clubs — two events naming his club `kick:ambiguous`, none `kick:club-unwitnessed`, another stem certified `kick:no-event` why `stem`. A hit of his own stem with no stated instant is untouched (every other sport's rows). Refuses only; the census carries the guard's reading under `yn_c7.wording`.

The hit is `matched_by premap_kickoff`, source `premap`, and carries `game_start` (the ISO minute), `witness` (the identical club's row), `club_by_exclusion`, `code_pair`, `league_alias por->ligpor`; `resolve` returns them; `resolve_explain` carries `yn_c7` (`his_game_start`, `why`, `kick_keys`, the arm's trace under `arm` — beside the names lane's `yn:names-unwitnessed` it rides `yn_c2.kick`, and `names_resolve` still reads the stored title after it — the family ride under `c3`, and on the lookup path `at_instant`). Read in this one call: never stored, never reused for another instant, never a table (pinned: no code or club literal, no `ordering` / `alias` / `safeName` in the arm's source). Behind `PREMAP_YN_IDENTITY` like all of C2–C6: off, nothing is read and every row answers as before.

**The census** (`explain_unmapped`: `no_side_match:kick:…`, or at the lookup `no_key_intersection:kick:…`): where nothing on his DATE carries his codes (§16.2's `venue:league-unlisted`), what the venue lists at his INSTANT names the class — games at his kickoff and none naming a club of his: `kick:club-unwitnessed` (the Liga Portugal shape: the venue dates the game a day earlier AND names both clubs otherwise); his instant unread: `kick:unknown`; nothing at his instant: unlisted stands. His instant unread with the venue's soccer rows on the board through another key and the arms before C7 empty: `kick:unknown`.
