## 33. E12 (2026-09-08) — the mirror follows his flow from first sight; the block he built before we saw the market is never bought at his newest cent

Owner ("How do we make sure we go up when he goes up and we go down when he loses. I want this to be proportional and ensure that we aren't screwing ourselves in the management of the same trades"; "10% of what he puts on everything he takes"; buys within 1c of him). The 03:22Z paired day (hard2/hourly_0322.log) read our average cost far above his on the losers we opened late — Khantiw/Hillel ours 0.670 vs his 0.477 (we lost 1,083.39 at −100 %, he −70 %), Richard/Kitthac 0.71 vs 0.287, Olivar/Grohbr 0.73 vs 0.298, Lorenz/Mauric 0.91 vs 0.336; the same shares at his average would have lost 562 less across 29 markets, 27 % of the set's loss. The cause, by the code: at open the plan sized the FULL ratio × his net (`rules.open_ratio`, `mirror_target`, `mi.target_shares`' `raw = ratio * net`) with no time or price term and priced it at his NEWEST fill's cent (`_his_level` keeps his most recent BUY of the long token; `buy_price(his_level, bid)`; `MIRROR_TAKE_AFTER_S` 0 lifts the offer at once), so a block he built at 0.29 was bought by us at 0.71 the moment the market mapped; his average cost was read nowhere in the sizing path. docs/mirror-to-a-tee-program.md decision 13 names that option (B) — "(A) follow only his flow from first sight (Rule LE, pro-rata ratchet); (B) build r × his_net into his open book at his newest level (today's code); (C) follow flow and rest a build leg only at ≤ his venue basis … RECOMMENDATION: (A) now (two numbers and a ratchet, no venue read); (C) after Phase 1 reads avgPrice semantics; (B) never" — and this change is (A) with option (b)'s small allowance. **THE RULE.** (1) AT OPEN (`_tick_candidate` → `_open_flow`) the tick's fills are split at the ingest clock (`mi.fill_clock`: `detected_at`, else the fill's stamp; an unclocked fill is old) against `now − FIRST_SIGHT_S` (two full ticks, `2 × POLL_S` = 60 s, derived and never a knob): THE BLOCK is his net on the book's long-token axis less the fills inside the window (`mi.pre_existing_block`, clamped to the axis — never past his net, never across zero), and the book records it as `mirror_books.flow_base` beside `flow_last_net` (his net it was read at), migration 057 (two nullable DOUBLE PRECISION columns, no DEFAULT, `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`; `backend/start.sh` applies the sorted glob on the API's boot, best-effort). The whole-net target is still what ADMISSION judges (`target_zero`, the game's room, the short share cap, every refusal as before); what the book OPENS at is ratio × his FLOW — `mi.flow_net` = his net less the block on the block's side of zero — sized by the same `mirror_target` at the same cap, so a market he built before we saw it opens with NO order (target 0, the book waits: `flow_wait` on the plan, the flat clock held off in `_maybe_close_episode` on the read and on E6's quiet skip alike, since he has not left) and his fill that woke the market is answered at his cent exactly as E4/E9 place it. Census `open_flow_only`. (2) THE CATCH-UP ALLOWANCE (`rules.open_catchup`, the plan row's `catchup` = `{vwap, mark, tol, allowed, flow_base, why}`): the block IS bought — the book opens on his whole net as before, `flow_base` 0 stored — when the mark is within `MIRROR_CATCHUP_TOL_CENTS` (2.0; `capped_env`, the environment may only lower it, 0 is never) of his cost over the block (`mi.vwap_of`: his size-weighted BUY price on the axis over the block's fills — the long token at p, on a short book the other token at 1 − p), `why` `within_tol`; or when the book's ratio is 1.0, the exact copy under `MIRROR_SMALL_BET_USD` (`small_bet`: the premium is cents on under $10, whatever the tolerance). `no_block` (nothing pre-existing: flow is his net; neither name counted), `tol_zero`, `vwap_unread`, `mark_unread`, `block_unread` (his whole net stands in: nothing bought) and `flow_only` are the other verdicts, each fail-closed toward NOT buying. Census `open_catchup` on `small_bet` / `within_tol`. (3) THE RATCHET, in D25's words, which win over the brief's peak-based formula: "`block_t = block_{t-1} × net_t/net_{t-1}` when net falls, unchanged on increases, 0 on a crossing to ≤ 0" (`mi.pre_existing_ratchet`, read on the block's axis; `flow_last_net` is net_{t−1}), run in `_tick_book` after the ratio step and BEFORE the target, written to the row (`_SQL_BOOK_FLOW`) before the in-memory book moves (a failed write ratchets nothing: `book_error`), the plan carrying `flow_base`, `flow_net` and `flow_ratchet = {from, to}` when it moved. So his 25 % sale is our 25 % reduce at his price within `MIRROR_EXIT_TOL` (block 10,000 under a net of 11,000 and our 100: he sells 2,750 → block 7,500, flow 750, target 75, a SELL of 25 taken at the bid within the cent), his full exit is our full exit (a crossing: block 0), an increase after a reduction leaves the ratcheted block where it is (the brief's `his_net / his_peak_net` would restore its share; the doc's words are what is built), and the sign flip keeps its rule (a crossing zeroes the block; the flow target is his whole net's sign; `sign_flip` flattens). A NULL block — every book opened before 057, or opened while the columns are absent — is the old rule, byte for byte: no open book changes behaviour retroactively. (4) THE COLUMN, read the 050 way: one probe per tick (`_SQL_FLOW_GUARD`, `_flow_guard`, before step O reads any book row, in the full tick and the fast tick); absent, `t.flow_col` False sends the 056-shaped book reads (`_SQL_BOOKS_OPEN_056` / `_SQL_BOOK_READ_056`) and the 056-shaped INSERT (`le._open_mirror_book` sends `_MIRROR_BOOK_INSERT_FLOW_SQL` only when handed a block), every book is an old-rule book, the heartbeat says `flow_column_absent` and the worker logs once per process; any other failure of the probe refuses the tick by name, `flow_guard_unreadable` (status degraded; the fast tick skips every woken market under it), because a blip read as absence would size every flow-only book on his whole net for a tick — the catch-up this change exists to stop. (5) THE SHADOW'S INSTRUMENT: `_shadow_check` is handed the whole-net arithmetic at the cap the book was sized at (`whole`), so `shadow_live_disagree` keeps comparing the two lanes' `ratio × net` and never trips on a book with a block (D25 warned it would; the shadow itself is not in this change and still measures his whole net). The three census names sit before E9's four (the E9 pin holds `keys[-8:-4]`; the brief's "before E7's pair" yields to it), past the served prefix, `integ` untouched. **The fold (2026-09-08; the review's HIGH-1, HIGH-2, MEDIUM-1, MEDIUM-2, MEDIUM-3, LOW-1).** HIGH-1, THE LATE ROW: `mi.fill_clock` alone (the ingest's `detected_at`, else the stamp) read a backfilled or reconciled OLD fill as the flow that woke the market — `ingestion/history.py` stamps `detected_at` at the backfill and an S1 reconciliation row is stamped at its insertion — so a 4,000-share fill of his stamped two hours ago and ingested ten seconds ago sat inside `FIRST_SIGHT_S` and the book opened on it at his newest cent (block 10,000 of his 15,000, target 500: the 4,000 bought by the back door). Now a fill is FLOW only when BOTH clocks say so (`mi.is_flow`): its ingest clock inside the window AND its own stamp not older than the window's start by more than `mi.LATE_FILL_S` = `ingestion/shadow_v2.LATE_POLL_ROW_S` (900 s — the poll lane's own late-row allowance, "detected_at − ts beyond this = reconciler artifact", reused rather than a second number for the same fact: the poll lane's ~281 s lag stays flow, a reconciler's or a backfill's row is the block whatever its ingest clock; an unstamped fill is the block; an unreadable allowance admits nothing past the window's start). The block's cut and the vwap's are the one predicate, so the late row's price enters his cost over the block (14,000 at 0.3014 on the review's world, the book opening on the 1,000 he added as we looked, 100 at his cent). HIGH-2, THE RATCHET ON HIS FILLS' AXIS: D25's one-way ratchet ran on `_net_for`'s two-source net (the per-market read when fresh, else the walk, else his fills; the smaller of two on a drift refusal), whose sources flip tick to tick — a 0.9 % reading wobble converted block into flow and the next tick BOUGHT part of the block with no fill of his (block 9,909, a BUY of 9 at his cent, compounding per wobble), and one zero reading (D1's merged pair) wrote block 0 to the row, the zero read's own flatten sold our 100, and the next tick bought his WHOLE block at his newest cent (1,100 at 0.71). Now the ratchet moves ONLY on a fall his FILLS witness: `net_t` and `net_{t−1}` are his fills' net (`mi.his_net` over `net_positions` of the fills the tick holds; `mirror_books.flow_last_net` stores the fills' net from the open on, and the block at open is cut from it), which moves only when a fill of his is ingested and falls only on a SELL of the long token or a BUY of the other — the reduction is by the fills' arithmetic, `net_t = net_{t−1}` less what they carry — and the flow target is sized on the same axis. The tick's reading is compared with it (`mi.flow_reading`) and NAMED on the plan when it disagrees by more than the D1 dust (one share, `mi.VENUE_LEDGER_TOL_SHARES`, the tolerance `_fresh_agreed` reads the same pair at — D1 proved the collapsed fills equal the venue's snapshot to the share): `flow_reading = {why: flow_reading_disagree | flow_reading_zero, reading, fills}` — no ratchet, no write, the book planned on the fills' net. So the wobble buys nothing (target 100, held, nothing written on either tick); the zero reading with no reducing fill of his writes NOTHING and sells nothing (the block stands, `flow_reading_zero`, the next tick at 11,000 buys nothing); a reading 3.6 % OVER the fills (inside `MIRROR_DRIFT_MAX`, which sized a BUY of 40 his fills never made) buys nothing; his real 25 % sale witnessed by his SELL ratchets 25 % and reduces 25 % at his price within `MIRROR_EXIT_TOL` whatever the venue reads that tick — agreeing, lagging at the old net, or zero (the fill is in: the ratchet moves, the reading is named, the reduce is the same); his full exit witnessed is our full exit (a crossing, block 0, the flatten at his price). The cost, stated: a sale the fills have not yet ingested is answered when they are, at his price (E4's exit), at most the poll lane's lag later — and when BOTH live lanes miss his SELL, the S1 reconciler's (`RECON_VENUE_LAG_S`, the 600 s margin, `ingestion/s1_emitter.py:111`; the re-review's LOW-1: a full exit the fills never carry is not a confirmed vanish on either rule, so the wait is the reconciler's, not a defect); a blip never sells and never re-buys. The old rule's readers keep the reading (the drift refusal on increases, the vanish confirmation, the freeze, the shadow's whole-net figure), a NULL block is untouched (the smaller of two readings still sizes it), and the fast tick's plan is the same `_tick_book`. No census name was added: the verdict lives on the plan row, the served prefix stands. MEDIUM-1, FIRST SIGHT IS THE WINDOW BEFORE THE OPEN, not before our first look: any deferral past two ticks — an `unmapped` or no-mark memo, a read the walk's cap deferred (`map_reads_capped`, `capped_tick`), `game_unreadable`, a loss stop — turns flow we could have seen into the block, by design under (A): the mirror buys the flow it could have answered, never his history at the current price, and the 2c allowance admits the rest. The number: on the 03:22Z paired day his fill reached our first order after more than 60 s on 9 of 29 books (105, 107, 113, 135, 165, 203, 247, 437, 1,315 s), and each of those opens is a flow-only open at 0 unless the allowance admits it (pinned: every fill 3 min old → the whole net is the block, 0 at 3c and the full catch-up at 1c; twenty adds at 30 s → the last two are flow). A cut at the candidate's first memo or stamp instead of `2 × POLL_S` is a follow-up, not this change. MEDIUM-2, THE SIGN-FLIP REOPEN IS FLOW-ONLY: the flip's close needs the venue read at 0 (the next tick) and the reopen the tick after (`book_seen`), so his crossing fills are at least two ticks old at the reopen and are the new side's block — target 0 unless the mark is within the allowance of his cost on the new side (the short axis reads his other-token BUY at 1 − p: −500 at a vwap of 0.70 is `within_tol` at 0.71 and `flow_only` at 0.74). Acceptable under (A); said here and in the module paragraph. MEDIUM-3, A FOLLOW-UP at the fold, built by E12b (the addendum below): `mi.vwap_of` read BUYs alone (the long token at p; a short's other token at 1 − p), so a long he built by SELLING the other token read `vwap_unread` and the book opened flow-only even at the exact mark — fail-closed, a missed follow, not money. ## 34. E13 (2026-09-08) — a book ends when ITS venue market has ended; the resolution sweep never starves

Owner (standing: "I want this to be proportional and ensure that we aren't screwing ourselves in the management of the same trades"). The 10:4xZ read (hard2/reslag_1041.log, frozen_detail_1035/1037.log): a mirror book ended on exactly two signals — step M reading the market's GAMMA row closed or resolved (`_SQL_MARKET`, keyed by his global condition_id), or the standing row settling from the venue's POSITION_RESOLUTION activities (`_settle_pmus_from_venue`) — and neither fires for a FLAT book whose gamma row nobody re-asks. `analytics/resolution.unresolved_traded_condition_ids` selected `DISTINCT t.condition_id ... LIMIT 500` with NO ORDER BY over 49,213 unresolved traded conditions (13,114 RN1's): the same 500 every cycle, the analytics beat at `newly_resolved: 4` per five minutes, gamma rows of markets played 09-04..09-07 still closed=f resolved=f with `updated_at` at the event day (the metadata refresher keeps ACTIVE markets only). Live: 40+ books opened 09-06/09-07 with ledger 0 stood 'live' (`books_live` 90), each costing the tick a standing-row read, a fills read and a market read and holding its asset claim; book 455 (order 2475 venue-cancelled, ledger 0) stood frozen cancel_pending since 06:52Z behind the W1 memo's early return (`no_mark` / `venue_terminal`) with nowhere to thaw; book 43 (short 1,042) stood frozen venue_ledger_disagree under the same lag (its P&L is the settle's). **LANE A — THE VENUE'S OWN TERMINAL STATE ENDS A FLAT BOOK.** The book's own quote read records `r.venue_state`; `_memo_terminal_book` memoises a state in `ms.STATE_TERMINAL` (EXPIRED / CLOSED / TERMINATED / the closing auction) for `UNMAPPED_TTL_S` (900 s). Now two terminal reads at least `UNMAPPED_TTL_S` apart — the memo write and the re-read after the memo's TTL, ~15 min for a truly ended market, plus one tick — with no non-terminal read between CONFIRM the state (`_terminal_book_seen`: the first read's instant of the run; `_terminal_book_confirmed`: the state the confirming read carried), and in step M (BEFORE the memo path's early return and before the frozen branch, so 455's shape ends too) a confirmed state on a FLAT book (|ledger_net| < FLAT_TOL_SHARES) is `closed_read`'s twin: `_SQL_BOOK_STATE(..., 'closing', 'venue_market_ended')`, `_mirror_stop('venue_market_ended')` (the one new census name, before `registered_no_increase`), the rest cancelled under that name, `plan.kind = closing` with `plan.venue_terminal = <state>`, `_maybe_close_episode` handed market_live False exactly as the gamma path hands it — 'cancelled' when the book never bought, 'cashed_out' when it did; a book with an order still non-terminal is cancelled first and closed the next tick (`orders_open`). The gamma path keeps `market_closed` byte for byte and wins the name when both read. NEVER on one read (the U10 review's 12:32Z expiries were transient); a non-terminal or unread state (OPEN, HALTED, SUSPENDED, PREOPEN, None) on the re-read CLEARS the pending and the confirmed state — the market is live again, nothing closes; a second terminal read inside the TTL confirms nothing; `_memo_terminal_book` keeps its guard (nothing written while an order is open); a book with shares HELD is never touched by the venue's word (`_venue_market_ended` is None on it: the settle closes it, the rules' 'held' verdict) and a frozen held book keeps its E5 frozen exit. A FROZEN book's ledger is not its position (review F1: book 77, ledger 0 while the venue held the register's 1,128; a placement_lost book whose lost BUY the venue filled and the ledger never booked — both 'flat' to the ledger, both closed 'cancelled' on the venue's word before the fold, the E5 frozen exit gone and the venue's shares settling into no row of ours), so `_venue_market_ended` is None on a frozen book whose `frozen_reason` is one under which the venue may hold shares the ledger does not (`_VENUE_MAY_HOLD_REASONS`: venue_ledger_disagree, placement_lost, lost_ambiguous, order_lost, wrong_sign_trip), and None unless the frozen book's last plan carries the venue's own position (`venue`, written by every read tick) as a number that is flat (|venue| < FLAT_TOL_SHARES) — absent, non-numeric or held: the book waits for the settle; a frozen cancel_pending / order_state_unknown / row_not_live book whose last read saw the venue flat (455's shape) still ends here. The confirmation persists across restarts beside the E6 memo under its own key, `mirror_terminal_confirm` (`[[whale, cid, seen_at, state | null], ...]`, the same writer, cadence and bound; the E6 key's value is pinned byte for byte by its tests, and the row's last_plan is rewritten by five paths — so a sibling key, never a column, never a knob), read at the same boot read (a future or unreadable `seen_at`, a non-terminal state: dropped, never guessed), forgotten when the book closes. Every new verdict fails closed toward NOT closing. **LANE B — THE SWEEP CHASES THE DESK'S MARKETS FIRST, THEN NEWEST FIRST, AND ROTATES.** `unresolved_traded_condition_ids(limit)` (signature kept) returns, de-duplicated: (1) every unresolved condition a `mirror_books` row with state <> 'closed' or a `live_orders` row with status IN ('submitting', 'filled', 'exiting') references — books first, then rows, ALL of them, never cut by the limit; (2) unresolved traded conditions by newest trade `ts` DESC over the last `NEWEST_WINDOW_S` (14 days, on `trades_ts_idx`: the aggregate never scans the whole ledger); (3) a rotating window over the remainder ordered by condition_id from a cursor in `ingestion_state` key `resolution_sweep_cursor` (`{"cursor": <last condition_id swept>, "at": ...}`), advanced every call, wrapped at the end, absent / malformed / junk read as the start, a failed write retried. The CLOB fallback (`clob_batch=300`) reads the same function and gets the same order — the desk's and the newest asked by both passes; the rotation window it gets is the NEXT one (every call advances the cursor), so a rotation-only market gamma asked and could not serve waits for a later cycle's CLOB window (review F2, accepted: the windows drift across wraps, the desk's markets are asked twice every cycle, and re-asking gamma's window would halve the sweep's reach). COST per cycle: the desk's set plus `limit` (500) through the gamma batch at 40 condition_ids a chunk (two requests a chunk at most: ~26), then the fallback's one request per condition (300, as before). No migration. NOT done: the shadow lane, the settle, the metadata refresher, book 43's settle (the sweep now reaches its row; its close is the settle's). Pinned: `test_e13_venue_close.py` (the two-reads rule tick by tick, cancelled / cashed_out, 455's shape, 43's shape not closed with its frozen exit planning, the open order cancelled first, OPEN / None / HALTED / SUSPENDED / PREOPEN clearing, the gamma path byte for byte, persistence and the boot read, the fail-closed verdict, the census place; the sweep on a real Postgres: books, then rows, then newest, the rotation across calls and its wrap, de-dup, the limit, the junk cursor, the CLOB fallback's order).

## 35. PNL lane 1 (2026-09-08) — the catch-up allowance reads the side of his cost: at or better is admitted at any distance

Owner (~11:50Z): "Fix all 3 of these immediately. I want to know when he makes money we make money. This needs to be right." hard2/PNL_program.md lane 1: E12's `open_catchup` (§33) judged the block by `abs(mark - vwap) <= MIRROR_CATCHUP_TOL_CENTS`, so a mark 5c UNDER his cost on a long was refused exactly as a mark 5c over — book 266 (his vwap over the block 0.503-0.549, the mark 0.50, |m − v| up to 4.9c > 2c) would have opened flow-only although the mark was BETTER than his cost, and the program reads ~$8k/day of target refused by the first-sight block (502 alone $2,500). Proportionality is the objective: a better mark carries all of his edge and no premium. THE RULE (`analytics/mirror_live_rules.open_catchup`, same five arguments plus `short`, the book's axis, handed by `mirror_live._open_flow`): a mark at or better than his cost ON THE BOOK'S AXIS is admitted at ANY distance — long `mark <= vwap`, short `mark >= vwap`, contract space (the long token's price, the axis `mi.vwap_of` reads both tokens onto: his BUY of the other token at 0.72 is 0.28 on a short) — `at_or_better`, allowed, flow_base 0, the book opening at ratio × his whole net capped by the game cap as before E12 (`_mark_at_or_better`, equality at the tolerance's 1e-9). The WORSE side is a tolerance: E12's 2c (`within_tol` / `flow_only`) in part (a), widened to D1's band in part (b) below. The allowance is NOT a tolerance: it is judged before `tol_zero`, so `MIRROR_CATCHUP_TOL_CENTS` lowered to 0 (capped_env: the env may only lower the worse side) still admits the better side and refuses every worse mark. FAILS CLOSED: an axis handed in that is not True / False is `axis_unread` (allowed False, flow_base = the block: nothing bought); an off-ladder or NaN mark on the better side is `mark_unread`, not admitted; `block_unread` / `no_block` / `small_bet` / `tol_zero` / `vwap_unread` / `mark_unread` / `within_tol` / `flow_only` and their order unchanged (`axis_unread`, then `at_or_better`, sit after `small_bet`, where the mark is first compared to his cost). The E12 five-argument call — no axis — keeps E12's symmetric verdict byte for byte (never wider), so the E12 and E12b pins stand unchanged. Census: an at-or-better open counts under `open_catchup` (a block bought) beside `small_bet` and `within_tol`; no new census name (no new refusal reaches the census — `axis_unread` is a plan-row `why`, the block never bought, like `vwap_unread`). Untouched: the ratio fixed at open, D25's pro-rata ratchet, E12b's witness, the whole-net target at `mirror_live.py` (E1's game cap), `mi.vwap_of`. Pinned: `test_e12_catchup_side.py` (long 5c under / equal / 1c over / 3c over; short 5c over / 3c under / 1c under / equal; the axis decides not the distance; `axis_unread` on None and every non-bool; `vwap_unread`, `small_bet`, `block_unread`, `no_block`, NaN / off-ladder `mark_unread` unchanged with the axis; tol 0 still admits the better side and refuses the worse; the order of the verdicts; the five-argument call is E12's; `_open_flow` hands the axis and admits 266's block at the whole-net target, a short's on its axis, the old-rule book untouched).

**Part (b) — the worse side's band (OWNER DECISION D1 = YES, 2026-09-08 ~13:3xZ: "make the changes, and get it live and running immediately"; hard2/PNL_program.md §2).** With the axis handed in, the WORSE side's allowance is no longer the flat 2c but `min(MIRROR_CATCHUP_MAX_CENTS, max(MIRROR_CATCHUP_TOL_CENTS, MIRROR_CATCHUP_PCT × vwap))` cents on the contract price on the axis (`mirror_live_rules._catchup_band_cents`): 10 % of his cost, never under E12's 2c floor, never over 5c — a 0.20 cost keeps 2c (10 % = 2c, as today), a 0.29 cost gets 2.9c, a 0.50 cost 5c (10 % = 5c, the cap), a 0.80 cost 5c not 8c. The premium is bounded by 5c × shares (≤ $125 on a $2,500 position) and the bought block earns his ROI on the day (+0.8 % to −9.9 % measured, §2). A mark past the floor but within the band is admitted, allowed, flow_base 0, named `within_pct` (`within_tol` stays for the 2c floor); past the band `flow_only` as before. `MIRROR_CATCHUP_PCT` (0.10) and `MIRROR_CATCHUP_MAX_CENTS` (5.0) are both `capped_env` floor 0: the environment may only LOWER the band (PCT 0 = the floor alone; MAX 0 = no worse side at all; MAX under the floor narrows the band under it; PCT 0.5 or MAX 9 from a shell stay 0.10 / 5.0). `MIRROR_CATCHUP_TOL_CENTS` at 0 is still `tol_zero`, judged AHEAD of the band — "never catch up" closes the band too; lowered under its 2c default it IS the band (TOL 1 admits 1c on every cost, never max(1c, 10 %) — the review fold below). FAILS CLOSED: the band is granted only with the axis (the E12 five-argument call keeps the flat 2c, so a 0.50 cost 4.9c over stays `flow_only` without the axis and the E12 / E12b pins stand); a NaN / None / string vwap is `vwap_unread` before the band is read; a knob that does not read (never from `capped_env`) grants no widening — the band is the tolerance alone. The plan row keeps its six keys; its `tol` is the floor knob, the band being `min(5, max(tol, 10 × vwap))` of the row's `vwap`. Census: a `within_pct` open is a block bought and counts under `open_catchup` beside `small_bet` / `within_tol` / `at_or_better`; no new census name. The whole-net target is still min(room, the $2,500 game cap) at `mirror_live.py` (E1); D25's ratchet and E12b's witness untouched; `at_or_better` / `axis_unread` / `vwap_unread` / `mark_unread` / `small_bet` / `block_unread` / `no_block` unchanged. Re-pinned: one E12 review pin (`test_e12_review_pins.test_q1_addendum_a…`) whose first world had the mark 3c over a 0.61 cost — 5c band under D1, so `within_pct` — now reads 6c over and says why; `test_e12_catchup_side` re-pins 266's block 3.1c over its 0.549 cost as `within_pct` at the whole-net target and 5.1c over as `flow_only`. Pinned (`test_e12_catchup_side.py`, part (b)): the knobs' defaults, `capped_env` source and `__all__`; the band pure; a 0.50 cost admits 4.9c / exactly 5c and refuses 5.1c; a 0.20 cost keeps the 2c floor; a 0.80 cost capped at 5c; env PCT 0.05 narrows to 2.5c, PCT 0 the floor alone, PCT 0.5 stays 0.10; env MAX 3 caps at 3c, MAX 1 narrows under the floor, MAX 0 closes the worse side, MAX 9 stays 5; the short's mirror on the worse side (under his cost) at every band, and the same marks on the long axis `at_or_better`; the better side unaffected by every knob; NaN vwap `vwap_unread`; tol 0 `tol_zero` ahead of the band; the five-argument call flat 2c; unreadable knobs no widening; `_open_flow` admits a 0.50-cost block 4.9c over at the whole-net target on both axes and refuses 5.1c at 0; the census source; this section. **Review fold (2026-09-08).** The lane's adversarial review found one latent fail-open at the money function and two at the rule — `_open_flow`'s refused-path clamp read the AXIS argument as a bool, so an `axis_unread` verdict on an axis whose truthiness disagreed with the target's sign handed back the whole-net target (never reached live: `_tick_candidate` hands `target < 0`); an off-ladder finite vwap (1.5, 55.0, a negative) was read as his cost and the better side admitted any mark against it where E12's symmetric tolerance refused; and `MIRROR_CATCHUP_TOL_CENTS` lowered from the environment (the pre-D1 handle on the worse side) had become the band's floor, so TOL 1 still admitted 4.9c over a 0.50 cost. All three are closed fail-closed toward not trading: `axis_unread` sizes 0 outright and the clamp reads the target's sign; his cost must read `0 < vwap < 1` or it is `vwap_unread` on both sides; a tolerance under its 2c default (`_CATCHUP_TOL_DEFAULT_CENTS`) is the band itself, never widened by the percentage — the env may only lower. The short's percentage read on the axis price (10 % of 0.80 = 8c capped 5c, 25 % of the 0.20 he paid) is documented as the program's literal rule and accepted, bounded by 5c × shares. Pinned in `test_pnl_l1_review_pins.py` (the tick on both axes, the game cap, the unread axis at the worker's call, the band's float edges, the three findings) and the re-pinned TOL-1 block in `test_e12_catchup_side.py`.

## 36. E18 (2026-09-08) — the entry rest lives 45 s; the IOC re-reads the quote before the send; the row records the decision

Owner (~11:50Z: "Fix all 3 of these immediately. I want to know when he makes money we make money. This needs to be right"; PNL program lane 6, hard2/PNL_program.md). The 11Z window (hard2/hourly_1129.log, order rows 2697-2736; hard2/PNL_lane1_fill.md): increases 49 placed / 19 filled in 11Z, 234 / 72 = 31 % over 05-11Z, and 10 of the 24 increase rows were cancelled `replace` after lives of 14, 14, 15, 16, 19, 21, 27, 30, 64, 424 s (median 20 s) — his next fill moved the plan by a cent (`keep_or_replace`'s 1c clause) or by a few shares (the MIN_MOVE_FRAC clause) and every re-quote went to the back of the venue's queue. Book 533: 2725 5@0.43 lived 16 s → replace, 2729 23@0.48 15 s, 2731 23@0.48 30 s, 2734 23@0.49 FILLED after 100 s once left alone; 2788 16@0.67 18 s → replace, 2789 filled in 4 s. Book 278 (short): four rests at the SAME cent 0.62, qty 86 → 316 → 493 → 451, 0 filled until the cent moved. Book 509: the S4 cover's IOCs 2726 / 2727 expired 0 filled because the ask moved between the tick's quote read and the send (2730 filled 11 @0.62 a wake later). Nothing on the order row said which decision placed it, what the quote was at the SEND, or which fill of his it answered.

**The rest-life floor.** `rules.MIRROR_REST_MIN_LIFE_S = min_wait_env("MIRROR_REST_MIN_LIFE_S", 45.0)` beside `MIRROR_REST_TTL_S` — a WAIT, the environment may only LENGTHEN it (10 → 45, 90 → 90). `rules.rest_decision` (keep_or_replace's verdict with its cause; `keep_or_replace` returns the verdict alone, its signature grown by `min_life_s` and `entry`): an ENTRY rest (`stands` not True and `entry` not False — the worker passes the plan's leg action, so an UNPRICED reduce rest at the ask is an exit and never waits) younger than the floor is 'keep' when the side and the intent stand and the cent moved under `REST_MIN_LIFE_CENT_MOVE` (2c, a constant); a quantity-only move (278's 86 → 316) is 'keep' with `add_pending` = {qty, wire, since} on the plan, re-planned through the replace branch the tick the floor has passed; a 2c move, a side or intent change, the TTL (600 s wins whatever the floor), a plan under a share and every unreadable fact replace as before; a rest at or past the floor decides byte for byte as before. The plan names it: `decision: kept_min_life`, `rest_life` {age_s, floor_s, cent_moved}, census `kept_min_life`. Exits (`stands`, E4) untouched; the 12/h replace budget untouched; the take off a young rest still fires the tick the ask arrives. **Folded 2026-09-08 from the adversarial review (hard2/PNL_L6_review.md).** The keep has ONE direction — the mandate's reading, "entries at his cent" (the operator's call on the review's MEDIUM-2): a young rest stands over a sub-2c move only while its wire is at or UNDER his new cent on a BUY (at or OVER it on a SELL; the plan's wire is his cent, `buy_price` = floor-cent of min(his level, bid)) — the queue kept when his cent rose (533: 0.43 → 0.48 → 0.49) — and his cent moving past the rest replaces at once (`replace_cent`), as before the lane, so a rest is never left a cent past him for the floor's life. And `add_pending` carries GROWTH only (the review's CRITICAL-1): a quantity that FELL under the rest's leaves — his witnessed sale — replaces at once (`replace_qty`) at any age; before the fold the floor kept the oversized rest standing past his proportion for up to 45 s.

**The IOC re-reads the quote.** `_ioc_reread` in `_place_reserved`, before the room's READ (`_room_qty`), the row and the op — the re-read is an await and E2's "nothing between this read and the take" admits none between `_room_qty` and `_room_take` (the review's HIGH-1, folded 2026-09-08; before the fold it sat between the two, so two games' IOCs under the parallel walk could each spend the last clip): ONE paced read through the E11 gate (`_bbo`, charged to the tick's call budget `t.guard_calls` like a write) immediately before ANY IOC — the entry take (`take_first` and the take off a standing rest), the exit's take, the S4 cover — and the IOC goes only while `rules.at_or_through` still holds on the RE-READ. Withheld by name, the plan carrying the two quotes: `ask_moved` (a BUY: {ask_at_plan, ask_at_send, wire}), `bid_moved` (a SELL); `ioc_reread_capped` (an ENTRY's re-read would pass `MIRROR_VENUE_CALLS_PER_TICK`: no read, no IOC — an EXIT's re-read is made whatever the count, as every exit's op is, M-1); `ioc_quote_unread` (the re-read failed, came back empty, or came back WITHOUT the side the IOC needs — the ask on a BUY, the bid on a SELL; a level never read did not move, so it is never `ask_moved` / `bid_moved` (the review's MEDIUM-1, folded 2026-09-08): no IOC, never a send on the tick's stale figure). The entry's remainder rests as today (`_entry_take` rests the plannable quantity under `IOC_SKIPPED`); an exit's rest goes with the next tick's plan, as after an expired IOC. The co-held flatten IOC (`_flatten_send`) already reads the bid immediately before its send and is unchanged.

**Migration 059** (`059_mirror_orders_send_record.sql`): three nullable columns on `mirror_orders`, no DEFAULT, `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS` — `ask_at_send` (the re-read's ask before an IOC's send; NULL on a rest), `decision` ('rest', 'take', 'cover', 'exit_rest' at the INSERT by `rules.order_decision`; 'take_in_band' reserved for lane 8; on a replace cancel the cause by `rules.replace_decision`: 'replace_cent', 'replace_qty', 'replace_side', 'ttl', 'replace_unread' — written after the row is terminal by `_SQL_ORDER_DECISION`, the plan carrying it as `replaced`), `his_fill_id` (`_his_fill_id`: the newest fill of his the tick held on the market by stamp then id, else the prior plan's last `his_fills_seen` entry, else NULL). Read the 057 way: `_order_cols_guard` probes once per tick after the 050 and 057/058 probes (never asked with 050 absent); absent → the 050 INSERT, no decision write, `order_cols_absent` on the heartbeat, logged once; any other failure refuses the tick `order_cols_guard_unreadable`. NULL on every older row means nothing: no reader sizes, places or cancels on these columns — they are the record `fills-missed` / `latency-census` read.

Pinned (`backend/tests/test_e18_rest_life.py`, 22 tests): the rules at 20 s / 45 s / 46 s (1c keep, 2c replace, 278's growth with `add_pending`, side / intent / future / unreadable / under-a-share / off-cent / no_price as before, the TTL winning under a floor of 1e9, the caller's floor only lengthening), the exit rest replaced at once under `stands` and under `entry=False`, the constant's env range (10 → 45, 90 → 90, junk / inf / negative the default), the decisions' words; the worker keeping a 20 s rest over the wire's 1c move UP and replacing it at 50 s with `replace_cent` on the row (the 1c move DOWN replaced at once), the quantity keep with `add_pending` and the regrow `replace_qty`, the priced exit rest replaced at 20 s, `ttl` written by step O's cancel; the entry IOC withheld `ask_moved` with the rest placed and no op spent, sent with `ask_at_send` 0.30 / 0.29 recorded, refused `ioc_reread_capped` under a budget of 0 and `ioc_quote_unread` on an empty re-read, the exit IOC withheld `bid_moved` and re-read with the budget spent, the take off a standing rest re-rested when withheld; 059 absent → the 19-parameter INSERT, no decision write, logged once, the fast tick agreeing, 050 absent never asking; the probe failing otherwise refusing both ticks by name and the probes' order; the file's shape (sorted last, three statements, nullable, no default), pglast, the migration on a real Postgres twice with the 059 INSERT, the decision UPDATE and the 050 INSERT reading back; the census place (six names before `registered_no_increase`), every name emitted, this section. Adjusted for the floor: `test_mirror_live_rules.py`'s two young-age pins read at 100 s (their clauses unchanged); `test_e6_review_pins.py` / `test_e6_tick_budget.py` count the exit IOC's one re-read. The review's pins (`backend/tests/test_pnl_l6_review_pins.py`, 7 tests): the fallen quantity replacing at the rules and at the worker (his witnessed sale, no drift), the re-read before the room's read, the one-sided re-read named unread, the one-directional keep on a BUY and a SELL, the floor under the TTL, the short's add rest.

## 37. Lane M (2026-09-08) — the paired ratio, the mirror's own filled-vs-missed, the on_target causes, the dark window named, the catch-up side read

Owner (~11:50Z: "Fix all 3 of these immediately. I want to know when he makes money we make money. This needs to be right."). The PNL program's measure lane (hard2/PNL_program.md section 0): the paired-day totals at 11:29Z read our_roi -0.2473 against his -0.0991 on 27 settled books (2.5x worse) and a 7.1 % median stake fraction against the 10 % target — but the fraction's denominator was his GROSS two-sided dollars (book 278 bought 21,896 USD gross for a 21,196 net while we held 10.0 % of his shares; 266's 8,531 USD of other-token buys were his exit), so 3,674 USD of the 6,070 USD "gap" was arithmetic; the probe's FVM / PRICEFID lines exclude lane='mirror' so none of the mirror's ~234 increases were ever scored filled against missed; 50,696 USD of his 24 h on our markets read `on_target` with no order and no cause; 24 of the top-40 no-book markets (463,444 USD) got their FIRST candidate verdict after his LAST fill (a dark window, not a mapping gap); and lane 1's at-or-better share had nowhere to be read. **WORKFLOW ONLY** (render-ops.yml; no worker, no rule, no migration, no env, no new census name — nothing here places or refuses). **`paired-ratio`** (in the `hourly` after paired-day): the paired-day set (its `bk` / `his` CTEs verbatim) with his NET cost beside his gross — on the book's token axis (long_asset / other_asset; a BUY_SHORT book mirrors) his running net is +BUY long +SELL other -SELL long -BUY other, `his_net_peak` its max absolute, `his_vwap_adds` the size-weighted price of the ADDING fills only (mi.vwap_of, E12b: the long at p, the other at 1 - p; on a short printed as the short leg's price so peak x vwap is dollars on both), `his_cost_net` = peak x vwap; `share_frac` = our peak shares (peak_exposure_usd / our_avg, else the fill-weighted avg_px of our adding order rows) / his_net_peak; `stake_frac_net` = our_staked / his_cost_net; `roi_ratio` = our_roi / his_roi (NULL when his_roi is 0), `pnl_ratio` = our_settled / his_pnl; a market with his_net_peak 0 or vwap NULL prints NULL fractions, never 0; the totals row names the OPPOSITE books with their last reason and ends in ONE line (`PAIRED <n> mkts his_roi .. our_roi .. roi_ratio ..x same a/b opposite <books> share_frac_med ..% stake_frac_net_med ..% cents_over ..c lat_med ..s`). **`fills-missed`**: his ADDING fills on our markets (24 h, chain/s1 collapsed as fills-answered) joined to our first entry order within 120 s — the plan's his_fills_seen[].order when the entry carries one, else the first open/increase/take placed in [ts, ts + 120 s) — classed `filled` | `partial` | `missed_expired_ioc` | `missed_replace` | `missed_open` | `refused:<his_fills_seen name>` | `unseen` (no plan held the fill) | `order_row_unread`; every fill graded at HIS price on the book's leg against markets.resolved_prices (n, his_usd, n_resolved, roi, a normal-approximation ci95, lag) per class with an ALL row and per book; `band_c` over every entry order row = (ask_at_place - floor-cent of his_level) x 100 on a BUY_LONG, (ceil-cent of his_level - bid_at_place) x 100 on a SELL_LONG, with the share at-or-through / inside +1c / inside +2c and the fill rate inside vs outside 1c (the band's reachable share BEFORE lane 8); the entry fill rate by hour and kind. **`on-target-why`**: every `on_target` entry with no order attributed off the row that holds it — `block` (fill older than opened_at - FIRST_SIGHT_S with a non-zero flow_base), `game_cap` (the plan's game_cap, or game_room <= 0), `flow_hold`, `stale_snapshot` (|target_raw| < ratio x |his_net| by over half a share), else `unattributed`; the plan is the row's LATEST, so a cleared cause reads unattributed, never guessed; the ALL row is fills-answered's on_target dollars on the same window. **`cand-refusals`**: the top-40 no-book path carries `first_verdict` and `first_verdict_after_his_last` (NULL with no verdict), and a fourth statement subtotals every no-book market as `first_verdict_after_his_last` / `verdict_while_he_traded` / `no_verdict_in_24h` with dollars. **`flow-books`**: `catchup_side` = better | worse | unread off the plan's own catchup {mark, vwap} (a long is better at mark <= vwap, a short at mark >= vwap; either missing is unread) and a third statement with the block's shares and dollars per side (a short's dollars at 1 - vwap; our share at the book's ratio, uncapped by the game cap). Rails: read-only, statement_timeout as the siblings (paired-ratio / fills-missed / on-target-why TO 120000; the hourly TO 120000 HEAD 1500). Pinned: test_render_ops_hourly.py (the hourly is the EIGHT presets' SQL joined, regenerated), test_render_ops_flow_books.py (three statements, the side expression byte for byte), the help line regenerated from the case labels (test_e9_review_pins q8, test_e5_frozen_exits). NOT done: no production run of the new presets before landing (the workflow runs the branch's file; this lane never pushes) — every statement ran exit 0 on a local Postgres 16 with the 001-058 schema and a two-book fixture (a long and a short) whose arithmetic was checked by hand; the live proof is the hourly's `== paired-ratio` section after landing. **Fold (2026-09-08, the lane's adversarial review, pinned on a real Postgres by test_pnl_m_review_pins.py):** a SHORT book's peak shares are its stake over the leg's price (1 - avg_cost; rules.book_buy peaks a short leg at leg x (1 - avg)) per book, the market's peak the largest book's, never a sum; `his_vwap_adds` is the adds on the side the peak was reached on (a flipped market priced the other side's peak at the first side's vwap); `his_roi_net` / `roi_ratio_net` sit beside the gross ones; `band_c` rounds his cent in numeric before the floor / ceil (float8 floor(0.29 x 100) read 28, so a rest AT his cent read a cent wide); `fills-missed` takes the axis of the book whose window (opened_at - 120 s, closed_at) holds the fill, the later book when two do, else the latest; `on-target-why` judges `stale_snapshot` against the flow the target is sized on (the plan's flow_net, else his_net - flow_base) and names the flow-only case `block_flow`; `first_verdict_after_his_last` reads the first verdict ever, not the window's; and the ONE worker change of the lane: `_write_plan` carries the open's `catchup` verdict from the prior plan onto every plan it writes, so `catchup_side` reads on every read row of a book opened from that commit on; the quiet skip's plan (`_SKIP_CARRIED`, E6's pinned contract, not widened by this lane) does not carry it, so a book quiet-skipped before its next read loses the verdict and reads `unread` from there, and a book opened before the commit holds it on its first plan only. Accepted as the review said: `missed_open` also names rejected / lost / expired-GTC rows, ci95 is unweighted beside a dollar-weighted roi, the 120 s window can credit one order to a fill it did not answer, the order-id cast rides an unpromised AND order the worker's integer writes make safe, and a short's `mark_minus_vwap_avg_c` is positive when better.

## 38. E16 (2026-09-08) — the freeze reads twice; the frozen exit follows his witnessed sale; the D2 thaw ON (D2 = YES, 13:3xZ)

Owner (~11:50Z: "Fix all 3 of these immediately. I want to know when he makes money we make money. This needs to be right."; the PNL program, hard2/PNL_program.md lane 2). Evidence, book 266 (hard2/book_266_1152.log; hourly_1129 paired-day): our 1,940 @0.50 filled 22:39:50Z (10 % of his 19,400 at placement); the book froze `venue_ledger_disagree` on ONE positions walk that had not caught up with that fill (`abs(venue_int - explained) > VENUE_LEDGER_TOL_SHARES` on a single read); a frozen book never increases, so while his net went 19,400 → 30,900 by 22:40:34Z we held 1,955 ($571 of stake); he exited 9,085 sh 22:46–23:16Z buying the other token at 0.75–0.89 and E5's frozen exit refused `frozen_venue_unread` on every tick the walk was stale, so we held 1,955 to 0 (settled −977.65, −100 % against his −51 %: $479 of excess loss on one book). 5 books stood frozen at 11:29Z. **THE RULE.** (1) THE FREEZE READS TWICE. A LIVE book's first disagreeing venue read is a SUSPECT, not a freeze: `venue_ledger_suspect` on the plan (`{venue, explained, delta, at, walk_at, cached}`) and the census; the increase arm is held by name `venue_suspect_hold` (a resting add is cancelled under it; nothing is bought on a reading not yet believed; no S4 probe on it either); the exit and the reduce are planned exactly as a live book plans them, with the LEDGER in `mi.plan`'s venue seat (the reading is what is not trusted; the add is already refused above the plan). The freeze fires only when the NEXT FRESH walk disagrees again by more than the tolerance in the SAME direction (`_second_disagreeing_read`): a read is fresh when the tick walked the account itself — `t.walk_at`, stamped in `_tick` at step R beside E9's `_last_walk`; the fast tick plans on the cached walk (`walk_at` None) and can neither freeze nor clear, it carries the suspect forward with its `cached` count (two reads of one walk are one read). A fresh read that agrees CLEARS the suspect (the plan no longer carries it, the book is on target as before); an opposite-direction read is a new first read; a suspect book is never quiet (E6: its second read is next tick's, never the rotation's). The freeze itself is E5's byte for byte: `_freeze(venue_ledger_disagree, detail)`, the E5 detail on the plan and the recent row, `frozen_exit: {held: transition_tick}` on the freeze tick (review F1), the first read kept on the frozen plan for the record. `wrong_sign_trip` keeps its ONE-read trip (an inversion is not a lag) and a book already frozen re-freezes as before (V3-2). A suspect book does not END on the suspect tick: `_maybe_close_episode` returns `venue_ledger_suspect` while the suspect stands (the vanish flatten sold the LEDGER; a surplus the walk reported — a lost fill's shares — would be nobody's on a closed book, 77's shape before E5); the flat clock keeps running and the next walk decides: agreeing, the close as any tick's; disagreeing, the freeze and E5's exit on the venue's own position the tick after. (2) THE FROZEN EXIT ON HIS WITNESSED SALE (`_frozen_reduce_on_fill`). Every frozen plan now carries `fills_at` (`_frozen_clock`: the newest ingest clock among the fills the plan held, `mi.fills_clock`, E12b's reference rule; a witnessed sale the exit did NOT get out — refused, no bid, capped — keeps the prior clock so the next tick witnesses it again; a placed exit or nothing witnessed moves it). When E5's frozen exit cannot read the walk (`frozen_venue_unread`: the positions unread, his market unread, the co-hold unreadable) and the tick's fills hold a REDUCING fill of his on the book's axis clocked strictly after the last frozen plan's `fills_at` (`mi.reducing_since` — a SELL of the long token or a BUY of the other on a long book, the mirror image on a short), the reduce is sized as a LIVE book sizes it: `rules.mirror_target(ratio, the FILLS' net)` (less the block on a flow book: the plan's `flow_net`), with the LEDGER in BOTH of `mi.plan`'s seats — never the venue figure it cannot read — placed through `_act` as E5 places it (E4's exit: his price within `MIRROR_EXIT_TOL`, the take when the bid is inside the cent, the rest at his cent otherwise), the row marked `frozen_reduce_on_fill` (the same prefix every reader of the marker matches: the stand on a frozen book, the adoption — `_adopt_reason` keeps the row's own marker verbatim — the excess, the preset), census `frozen_reduce_on_fill`. Rails: never more than the ledger (`int(p.qty) > abs(ledger)` refuses), never an increase (`leg_action != reduce` → `frozen_reduce_only`), never past his proportion — a target at or above the ledger on the axis (our share already under his after his sale: 266's own shape after his 11,000 adds while frozen) is `frozen_witness.held = under_proportion`, no sale; no prior clock (`unclocked`, the first frozen plan), no reducing fill after it, a reducing fill older than the clock, an unreadable target or mark: refused `frozen_venue_unread` as before; a placement_lost book reduces the LEDGER's share on his sale, its surplus waits for E5's read. With the walk READ, E5's venue-sized exit runs exactly as before. (3) THE THAW — OWNER DECISION D2 = YES (2026-09-08 13:3xZ, owner and management: "make the changes, and get it live and running immediately"), ON BY DEFAULT. A HELD `venue_ledger_disagree` book (|ledger| ≥ FLAT_TOL_SHARES) whose venue read equals ledger + manual + registered within the tolerance on TWO consecutive FRESH reads (`venue_agrees: {reads, at, walk_at}` on the frozen plan, `_agree_record`: a cached read carries the count, a disagreeing tick drops the record so the count starts over) thaws to live — `_SQL_BOOK_THAW_AGREES` (state live, frozen_reason NULL, frozen_ticks 0, the one reason named in the statement), `thawed_venue_agrees` on the plan, the book planning live that tick — behind `rules.MIRROR_FROZEN_EXITS` (the E5 knob, env may only turn it off) AND `ml.MIRROR_FROZEN_THAW`, read once at import from `PMUS_MIRROR_AUTO_THAW` through `_auto_thaw_switch`: absent is ON (the default moved in CODE, as the discipline requires — the E16 build carried it as a False constant until D2 was answered); present, ON only for on/1/true/yes and OFF for any other word (off/0/false/no, a blank, a typo — stricter than `rules.env_switch`, whose typo keeps the default: here the default is the raised rail, so an unreadable word fails closed toward NOT thawing); a change in the environment needs a restart. A switch may turn a rail off, never raise one: nothing in the environment makes the thaw MORE than the code's default. Off, the book stays frozen by name: `thaw_held` = `thaw_off` (the switch or the E5 knob off) / `one_read` / `cached_read` (the fast tick's cached walk — which never plans a frozen book at all, `not_live`; the full tick stamps its own walk at step R), census `thaw_held`, its E5 exits untouched; the thaw is otherwise the E5 register (§25). D2's own bound: the thawed book is a LIVE book again under rule (1) — a fill the venue reports later is a suspect on its first fresh read and the freeze on the second, and the frozen exit follows him as before; a frozen book on his witnessed sale whose walk agrees twice thaws on the second walk and the LIVE path takes his sale (the same SELL at his cent, a plain `take`, never the frozen marker, nothing sold twice). The rule names ONE reason: a book frozen `placement_lost` / `order_lost` / `lost_ambiguous` is never thawed BY THIS RULE (no `venue_agrees` record, no `thawed_venue_agrees`); it keeps E5's own one-read thaw when the venue AGREES (the lost order the venue never filled: `test_a_lost_close_response_is_named_and_reconciled…`, `test_s4v2_a_lost_cover_is_adopted…` — the code and its pins win over the brief's "never auto-thaws"; a lost order the venue DID fill disagrees and never agrees on its own, so nothing is masked, and a fill the venue reports later re-freezes on two fresh reads). A FLAT frozen book under any reason thaws on the one agreeing read as E5 did (it closes as any flat book; nothing resumes). Every new verdict fails closed toward NOT trading; no new knob; the four census names sit before `venue_market_ended` (the E9 / E13 tail pins moved by four: −69 → −73, −70 → −74). NOT done: D2 = YES (the constant), lane 3's witnessed reductions on live books, lane 1's allowance, the preset columns (lane M), the placement_lost bound. Pinned (`backend/tests/test_e16_freeze_two_reads.py`, 22 tests): one disagreeing read a suspect (no freeze, no increase, the exit still plans on the ledger); two fresh reads in a row the E5 freeze with its detail; disagree-then-agree cleared and a third read a first read again; the cached second read never freezing and the fresh one after it freezing, the rule at the unit; the opposite direction a first read; the suspect book hot and its add rest cancelled by name; the wrong-sign trip on one read byte for byte; the suspect book flat on his vanish not closed on the suspect tick (agreeing → closed; disagreeing → frozen, then E5 sells the venue's 300); the thaw on two fresh agreeing reads with the constant on; a disagreeing read restarting the count; the default off (`thaw_off`, the exit still following him; the E5 knob off with the constant on); the constant code, never the environment; placement_lost never thawed by the rule; the flat book's one-read thaw; the witnessed 30 % sale with the walk unread → the reduce on the fills' net at his cent, marked, the clock moving, the next tick refusing as before; ratio × his sale and `under_proportion`; no reducing fill (and his ADD) refusing as before on every unread reason; a reducing fill older than the clock no witness and an old-stamped one clocked after it a witness; never past the ledger, never a BUY (the mutant refused `frozen_reduce_only`), the placement_lost surplus untouched; the exit that did not go out keeping its clock (the rest at his cent standing through the unread tick); the short book's cover on his witnessed buy-back; the census names, the marker's prefix and the docs. In `test_mirror_live_worker.py`, `test_e5_review_pins.py`, `test_e5_review_v3_pins.py`, `test_e9_review_pins.py`, `test_s4_review_pins.py`: every one-read `venue_ledger_disagree` world reads twice (the suspect asserted on the first read), the served-prefix pins moved by four, one E16 test emits the four names for the every-name census. **FOLDED (2026-09-08, the lane's adversarial review, hard2/PNL_L2_review.md: R1, R2, R4, R5).** A suspect record a CACHED read started is never the FIRST of the two reads (`_second_disagreeing_read`: a prior with `walk_at` None answers False, so the fresh read after it writes a fresh record and the one after THAT freezes); a tick whose frozen exit refused BEFORE the witness was read (`transition_tick`, `frozen_fill_this_tick`, `frozen_exits_off` — nothing witnessed because nothing read) keeps the prior plan's `fills_at`, and on the transition tick takes the newest clock among the last LIVE plan's `his_fills_seen` (`_frozen_clock`, `_seen_clock`), so a sale of his that landed in that tick is witnessed by the next unread tick instead of being consumed unanswered; `_venue_market_ended` answers None for a LIVE book whose last plan carries `venue_ledger_suspect` (the frozen clause's reading: the surplus the walk reported is nobody's on a closed book); and `under_proportion` ALONE cancels a standing frozen reduce under `frozen_venue_unread` (he re-bought past our proportion — E5 F4's rule, which the read path applies as `frozen_no_his_exit`), every read that FAILED keeping V3-3's hold. ## 39. E15 (2026-09-08) — a reduce on EVERY book needs his witness; the two-sided burst nets; the witnessed flip reopens at size; the drift his fills explain

Owner (~11:50Z: "I want to know when he makes money we make money. This needs to be right"); PNL program lanes 3+4 (hard2/PNL_program.md, PNL_lane2_sizing.md), task 69. The paired day's ROI gap was two mechanisms, not price: book 278 (book_278_1152) — nine increase rests at wires 0.62 → 0.12 → 0.07 over 21:04-21:17Z sized on a reading that lagged his 4,415 Yes @0.07-0.11 AND 1,414 No @0.885-0.92 bought inside one window, 24 @0.12 and 292 @0.07 FILLED = we sold Yes at 0.07-0.12 while his net was moving LONG, $446 of our $539 loss on a sale he never made; book 451 — an exact copy of 16 @0.39, he added to $46, the one-way ratio step took the target to 10 and the reduce path SOLD 6 at 0.33 while he was BUYING; books 198 / 122 / 451 — the sign-flip reopen sized 0 ($740 of stake) because the crossing fills were older than `FIRST_SIGHT_S` at the reopen (E12's MEDIUM-2, by design then); `drift` refused $14,254/6 h of his fills where the whole-book walk was simply older than the chain lane's rows. E12b's witness (the ratchet moves only on a REDUCING fill of his clocked after the reference) governed FLOW books alone; a book with no block sold on any fall of the target. **THE WITNESS ON EVERY BOOK.** Every plan row now carries `reduce_ref = {target, at}`: the target the last un-held plan sized and the newest ingest clock among the fills it counted (`mi.fills_clock`, E12b's clock, the ingestion host's own `detected_at` stamps — a fill the reference counted never witnesses it again, a fill ingested after it always can); a row written before this rule seeds it from its own `target` and `at` (a flow book from `flow_last_at` first: the E12b reference), a `no_plan` skip carries it, a never-planned book plans as today and its first plan writes it. In `_tick_book` (the fast tick is the same function) a `reduce` whose target FELL below the reference's on the leg (long: target < ref; short: target > ref; a reference of 0 — a flatten's, a flow-only open's — is no witnessed reduce at all, so every reduce against it needs his fill: a venue blip read at 0 and back is never sold into) goes out only when `mi.reducing_on` (E12b's `reducing_since` on the BOOK'S OWN leg — a SELL of the long token or a BUY of the other on a long, the mirror image on a short — over the fills clocked strictly after `reduce_ref.at`) is > 0; else the plan HOLDS at the ledger, `reduce_unwitnessed = {from, to, cause}` on the plan with cause `ratio_stepped` (451's shape: the $10 / $20 line), `short_share_cap`, `game_cap`, `reading` (the two-source net under his fills' net: 278's lagging walk, the smaller-of-two on a drift refusal, a zero read), `fills` (the chain-first collapse: the fills' net itself fell with no reducing row), `no_reference` (a plan with no readable clock: seeded at the ledger and the fills' clock, his next reducing fill witnesses), the reason `reduce_unwitnessed` when nothing of his exit rests, and — exactly E12b's construction — HIS witnessed exit already resting keeps its whole E4 life through `_act`: the plan handed over is `mi.plan` toward the REFERENCE's target (the last witnessed state), `cap` on the name, a reducing side only, the take the tick the bid arrives; nothing witnessed outstanding: nothing sent, a resting add cancelled under the hold's name (the target fell: nothing more is bought). A witnessed fall reduces to the target at his price within `MIRROR_EXIT_TOL` and the reference moves to his sale's clock — and the target of a witnessed fall on an old-rule book is sized on HIS FILLS' net (the witness's own axis, E12b's construction), never on the two-source reading: a walk lagging his adds would otherwise turn a witnessed 10 % sale into the reading's 30 % reduce (he sells 100 of 1,000 as we watch, the walk reads 700: 10 sold at his price, not 30; the reading's excess follows when the fills carry it) — only while the reading keeps him on the book's side: a reading at 0 or across it (the paired flatten of his merged pair, the confirmed vanish, the sign flip) keeps its own reader as before, so the S4 pin's unpriced paired flatten still covers the whole 300 once his buy-back prices it; a target at or above the reference's is the reference's own reduce (witnessed when it moved) and goes out as today. His witnessed FULL exit stays our full exit; `flatten_vanished` (the data API's confirmation) and the sign flip's `flatten_paired` keep their own readers and E12b's `flow_hold` keeps precedence on flow books — none of them is this rule's. **THE TWO-SIDED BURST (278).** `mi.burst_since`: his fills clocked after the reference carry an ADD and a REDUCTION on the leg at once (`adding_since` and `reducing_on`, each > 0) → `flip_burst_net = {adds, reductions, net}` on the plan and an old-rule book sizes THIS tick on his fills' net in place of the two-source reading (a flow book is on the fills' net already, `mi.flow_net`); one leg alone is no burst and, with the reading lagging his other leg, the reduce holds under `reading` until his fill lands — so 278's shape covers 203 of the short on the net of the burst (his -6,394 → -1,979 on the No axis) and never sells Yes at 0.07 on the No leg alone. **THE FLIP'S WITNESS.** At the sign flip, when `mi.reducing_on` over the fills after the reference is > 0, `flip_witness = {since: the reference's clock before the crossing, reduced, at}` rides on the plan and is carried while the flip stands, so the close tick (the venue read at 0) hands it to the reopen: `_tick_candidate` reads the last CLOSED book's plan on the market (`_flip_since`, `_SQL_BOOK_FLIP`; only a plan closed under `sign_flip` with a witness no older than `mi.LATE_FILL_S`, else None) and `_open_flow(..., since=)` cuts first sight at that clock — WIDENING the window only, never narrowing it — so the crossing fills, witnessed live, are the new side's FLOW (`catchup.flip_reopen = {since}`, block 0, `no_block`) and the reopen sizes ratio × |net| at once under the game cap and the short share cap (the harness: he sells 1,000 and buys 500 No as we watch; tick 1 flattens at his price and names the witness, tick 2 closes, tick 3 opens the short at -50). A flip no fill of his witnessed after the reference (a venue reading, fills older than the reference) carries nothing: the reopen is flow-only at 0, as today. **THE DRIFT HIS FILLS EXPLAIN (lane 4).** When the whole-book walk is the reading (`drift_src == 'book'`), older than one tick (`snap_age > POLL_S`) and refusing `drift`, and his fills' net stands ABOVE it on the leg by exactly the adds the chain / s1 lanes ingested after the walk's clock (`mi.drift_explained`: `adding_since` over chain / s1-sourced rows alone — the poll lane's rows never explain a reading, they are what the chain corrects — within the one-share dust), the INCREASE is sized on the fills' net, `drift_fills_explain = {snapshot_at, fills_after, delta}` on the plan and the rule's drift figure kept beside it; a fills' net UNDER the snapshot is never this rule's (a reduce never sizes on a reading), a rise the sourced adds do not explain to the share, a walk younger than one tick, a poll-only explanation, an unreadable figure: refused `drift` as today. Rails: every new verdict fails closed toward NOT trading; no new knob, no census name (the verdicts live on the plan row: `reduce_unwitnessed`, `flip_burst_net`, `flip_witness`, `drift_fills_explain`, `reduce_ref`); the per-market read (stamped this tick), the whole-net admission, the allowance, the take arm, `MIRROR_EXIT_TOL`, the freeze, E5's frozen exits, the mirror_orders INSERT and migration 059 (lane 6), `open_catchup` (lane 1), the standing-row branch (lane 5) untouched. The cost, stated: a fall of the target with no reducing fill of his holds our shares past his proportion until his next sale (at most the ingestion lag; the S1 reconciler's when both live lanes miss his SELL) — never a sale he did not make. The walk's clock is the wall clock at the tick's read (`_Tick.snap_read_at`, stamped in `_snapshot`) less the age `ms.snapshot_sizes` measured on the same wall clock — never `t.now` less the age, which drifts by the tick's elapsed time and would read an add the walk already counted as after it; a walk this tick did not stamp explains nothing. ## 40. E17 (2026-09-08, PNL lane 5) — a standing row retired on a live market re-anchors; a closed book's shares are adopted by the next episode

Owner (~11:50Z: "I want to know when he makes money we make money. This needs to be right"). Book 204 (hard2/book_204_1150.log): our 13 @0.60 filled 17:00:50Z (10 % of his 134.6); the book closed 18:48:01Z "closed: standing row settled" while the market was live; he added 6,350 sh after (net 6,485.3 at 22:52Z); every later candidate on the market was refused `venue_already_holds` (rules.admission) on our own 13 shares — $354 of his $3,892 on that book, 346 the same shape. **THE SIGNAL.** A mirror row leaves 'filled' by exactly three writers: `engine._settle_pmus_from_venue` writes `status='settled'` with `pnl` (the venue's POSITION_RESOLUTION realized for the slug) AND `settled_at` in ONE statement — the venue's own word; `le._close_mirror_episode` writes 'cashed_out' / 'cancelled' on a FLAT row (the mirror's own close); the copy lane's stale-exiting reaper writes 'cashed_out' + `settled_at` on an 'exiting' row (never a mirror row). So a 'settled' row carrying BOTH marks is the venue's settle; one carrying NEITHER is no writer this worker knows; one mark alone is `settle_marks_partial`. **STEP M** (`_standing_row_verdict`, before the close as before): a row 'settled' with neither mark on a book that HOLDS shares (|ledger| ≥ FLAT_TOL_SHARES) whose row shares are the ledger's (within `mi.VENUE_LEDGER_TOL_SHARES`) re-anchors ONLY when the gamma row reads closed=f resolved=f with resolved_prices NULL AND the venue's own quote read THIS TICK (one paced `_bbo`, `book=True`) is OPEN: `_SQL_STANDING_REANCHOR` sets the row back to 'filled' with its WHERE re-checking both marks (`raw.mirror.reanchored = {at, status_was}`), the plan names `standing_row_reanchored = {row, status_was, ledger, venue_state}`, the book stays live and is planned on its row next tick (`standing_row_reanchored` on the census). The venue's settle, a flat book, a 'cashed_out' / 'cancelled' row, a terminal reader (gamma closed / resolved / resolved_prices, the venue EXPIRED / CLOSED / …) close byte for byte as before with no new name; a reader that cannot tell — the gamma row unreadable, the venue state unread or not OPEN (HALTED / SUSPENDED / PREOPEN: not terminal, but no positive witness of a live market either), the row's shares not the ledger's, one settle mark without the other — is `standing_row_ambiguous` (its `why` on the recent list) and closes as before; an UPDATE that touches no row (the venue settled it between the read and the write, or any failure) is `standing_row_reanchor_failed` and closes as before. Every verdict fails closed toward the old close, which does not trade. **THE CANDIDATE** (`_tick_candidate`): the newest CLOSED book on the whale's condition that still holds shares is read with its standing row's marks (`_SQL_PRIOR_EPISODE`); `rules.AdmissionFacts.prior_episode_ledger` (appended LAST, None fail-closed) carries its ledger when it sits on the candidate's leg, and `rules.admission` lets `venue_already_holds` pass ONLY when `rules.prior_episode_adoption`: the venue's shares and the prior's ledger both numbers, the prior a held position, the same sign, |venue − prior| ≤ `mi.VENUE_LEDGER_TOL_SHARES`. Any other magnitude stays `venue_already_holds`. Then, in order: the prior's row carrying the venue's settle (both marks) refuses `adopt_prior_venue_settled` — the venue's word on the position stands and nothing reopens against it, whatever the walk and the gamma row say (named, so the shape's dollars are counted; 204's row carries settled_pnl 5.20 and takes this exit unless its marks are absent); a prior whose close clock is unreadable, whose row is not the mirror's own 'settled' one, or whose row shares are not its ledger refuses `adopt_prior_unreadable`; no fill of his that is flow on the CLOSE clock (`mi.is_flow(f, closed_at)`: ingested after the close, stamped no more than LATE_FILL_S before it) refuses `adopt_no_fill_since_close` — his older fills are the block (E12) and open nothing. Admitted, `_adopted_open` is `_open_flow` on the close clock: the block is his pre-close net less the part the adopted shares already cover (`rules.adopted_block`: adopted / ratio on the block's axis, clamped between 0 and the block; 134.6 − 13/0.10 = 4.6 on 204), the vwap over the block's fills, the same `open_catchup` verdict (the plan's `catchup.since` = the close clock), the same `mirror_target` at the same cap — so the game cap counts the adopted shares (a 60,000 flow at 0.595: the cap's 4,201 target, the increase 4,188). The open is `_open_adopted_book`: migration 014's one-fill-per-asset index holds 'settled' rows, so a second 'filled' row beside the prior's can never be inserted — the prior episode's OWN row is re-anchored to 'filled' (the same `_SQL_STANDING_REANCHOR`, its stamp naming `adopted_by`) and becomes the new book's standing row, the book's opening ledger is the adopted shares at the prior's cost (`_SQL_BOOK_ADOPT`, guarded on ledger 0), all in ONE transaction with the mirror_books INSERT (`le._MIRROR_BOOK_INSERT_*` by flow shape, episode n+1 as before) — any failure rolls every write back and is `open_failed:<Exc>` / `book_exists`, nothing half-opened. The first plan carries `adopted_prior_episode = {book, row, episode, shares, avg_cost, closed_at}`; census `adopted_prior_episode`. The seven names sit before `registered_no_increase` (the E12 pin holds keys[-12]; the E9 / worker-file tail pins −69 → −76, −70 → −77). No knob, no migration, no new column: `_SQL_STANDING_READ` reads `settled_at` beside `pnl`. NOT in scope, untouched: the settle sweep (a re-anchored row on a slug the venue later resolves settles as any row), E13's venue close (its tests pass unchanged), the frozen branch, the ratio and the exits. **THE FOLD (2026-09-08, the lane's adversarial review).** HIGH-1, the owner's mandate on our own dust (Martinez 12:10Z: the flip close's IOC bought 371.2 against 371 sold, the venue +0.2, the INTEGER ledger 0, every later fill of his refused): a venue residual that is non-zero and under `mi.VENUE_LEDGER_TOL_SHARES` (1.0), which the prior read cannot adopt, beside a CLOSED mirror book on the same whale and condition whose standing row is lane 'mirror' on one of the market's tokens (`_SQL_PRIOR_DUST_OURS` / `_prior_dust_ours`: identity, never a magnitude match) is the mirror's own dust — `rules.AdmissionFacts.venue_dust_ours` (appended LAST, default False, honoured only as the bool True through `rules.venue_dust_is_ours`, which also holds |venue| under the tolerance; admission's `vn != 0.0` itself unchanged) admits, the book opens at ledger 0 (the freeze reads |venue − ledger| ≤ 1.0 as agreement), the census names the event `venue_dust_ours` beside `adopted_prior_episode` (eight names now; the tail pins −76 → −77, −77 → −78) and the first plan carries `venue_dust = {venue, prior_book}`; any residual at or over 1.0, no such closed book, or the identity read failing is `venue_already_holds` as before. ## 41. E19 (2026-09-08, PNL lane 8) — the candidate opens on the SMALLER reading; the fill collapse keeps distinct fills; fills-vs-venue

Owner (2026-09-08: "Fix all 3 of these immediately. I want to know when he makes money we make money. This needs to be right."; 13:5xZ "Why did we only have 12c on Martinez. I see RN1 had 25000 on Martinez"). Market aec-atp-pedmar-frafor-2026-09-08 (hard2/book_534_1424.log, candref_1408.log, why_1408.log): book 529 LONG 23 @0.51 closed on his flip; book 534 SHORT to -371 as he went to -3,717; his flip 12:08:40Z; 534 closed 12:10:17Z on target 0 (flow_net 11,974.6, drift 0.523 against the venue's own per-market snapshot mkt_long 29,054.9 / mkt_other 3,950.8 / snap_net 25,104). The reopen was refused `drift` at 12:10:43Z with his fills reading 11,974.6 and the venue 25,104 -- BOTH LONG -- then `side_band` (the market ran 20c; by design, we do not chase), then `drift` on every later window (12:50:55Z 7,861.4 / 13:06:02Z 12,576.2 / 13:21:26Z 12,650.3 / 13:36:31Z 10,594.6) until `no_mark` and EXPIRED at 14:02:55Z. No book followed. THE CAUSE: the D1 collapse (section 8) dropped every per-match row whose (tx, asset, side) held a chain / s1 row, and one of his sweeps fills across price levels under ONE tx -- at 12:09:24Z s1 5,225 @0.62 beside poll 3,601 @0.61 and 5,245 @0.61; at 12:09:45Z s1 5,225 @0.61 beside poll 4,283.5 @0.60 -- so four real poll rows (18,374.5 sh, the shadow's `fills_dedup_shares` at 14:02:55Z) were dropped and his net read ~50 % low for the rest of the match. The venue's 29,054.9 is the SUM of every row (chain 230.4 + s1 15,695 + poll 13,129.5).

THE COLLAPSE KEEPS DISTINCT FILLS (`mirror_shadow.his_fills`): two rows are the SAME fill only when they agree on (whale, tx, asset, side) AND (i) the per-match rows under the key SUM to the net-leg row's size within max(0.01 sh, 0.1 %) AND their size-weighted price is the net-leg row's within HALF a cent (0.005: the chain average, rounded to the mil, IS its splits' -- Kostyuk's 4,996 + 5,172 + 4,996 = 15,164 at 0.5633 against 0.563 stays collapsed, the venue to the share; one poll row of the net leg's size a cent off is a second fill) or (ii) the row repeats a net-leg row's price to the cent and its size within the same dust. Rows that share a tx but differ in price or size are distinct fills and every one counts; 5,225 against 5,245 (20 sh, 0.38 %) is two fills. The key is the D1 key (a '' hash its own row; the hash lowercased; the side uppercased) -- nothing else, no synthetic tx, no detected_at window. Every reader (the candidate, the book, the shadow's `his_paired_sh` / `fills_dedup_*`, the report) reads the one function. The D1 review's pins that encoded the old rule ("a chain row wins even when the poll legs do not sum to it"; "the s1 same-asset deferral under-reads" -- that pin HELD the Martinez defect, 5,000 against the venue's 2,000) are re-pinned to the venue's figure; the randomized invariant is E19's rule in Python. The mutants (the sum widened to any sum; the repeat by price alone) are caught on Martinez's rows.

THE CANDIDATE OPENS ON THE SMALLER READING (`rules.smaller_reading`, `AdmissionFacts.drift_sized_smaller`, `_tick_candidate`): when the per-market read is FRESH (drift_src 'market') and `drift_net_rule` is past MIRROR_DRIFT_MAX, two readings of ONE sign -- his fills' net and the venue's per-market net -- size the target on the smaller magnitude (signed; the short mirror image the same rule), and admission is told so by an explicit fact beside the REAL drift number, never a stand-in: the drift clause admits past the max only with `drift_sized_smaller is True` AND `snap_market_fresh is True`. The plan's `drift_sized_smaller = {drift, drift_src, fills_net, venue_net, net, sized_from: 'smaller'}`; the refusal context (the ctx the walk hands `_note_candidate_refusal`) carries the same keys -- the row's columns are fixed, its `his_net` / `target` are the smaller reading's. The census counts `drift_smaller_open` at the open (placed before `registered_no_increase` by E13's convention; the tail pins moved by one). The refusal stays `drift` for: signs that disagree (the two-source reading sizes nothing: `target_zero` / `short_side_refused` before admission), either reading not a number, the read not fresh (the whole-book walk's 'book' never sizes it), the smaller rounding to nothing (`target_zero`). E12 then runs unchanged on that net: a mark within the catch-up tolerance opens on the whole smaller net (`open_catchup`), a mark past it flows only (`open_flow_only`). THE OPENING TICK: `_tick_book` plans the new book in the same tick and its increase gate read the same disagreement as `drift`, so the admitted open placed nothing; the gate now honours, on the opening tick alone (the in-memory `_drift_smaller` the candidate set, taken off by the first plan), the admission it was handed -- `net` there IS the smaller reading. Lane 34's drift section is byte-identical (pinned); a standing book under a persisting disagreement refuses its increases `drift` as today.

THE MEASURE (`fills-vs-venue`, render-ops.yml; unrun on the branch): per market he traded in the last 24 h with a mirror book, the raw rows / shares by source (chain, s1, poll+backfill), the collapsed long / other / net under the OLD key and the NEW key, both SQL-side, the venue's per-market figures from the newest book's last plan (mkt_long, mkt_other, snap_net, flow_net, drift, drift_src, its clock; the fills counted at or before it, `later_sh` beside), ordered by the NEW key's gap to the venue's net; then the 24 h totals (markets, raw, dropped under each key, the dollars the new key keeps, the gap under each, how many markets each key reads closer).

Rails: no knob (MIRROR_DRIFT_MAX stays `capped_env`), one census name for one new admission, every other verdict as before; the fact's default False, the rule None on anything but two numbers of one sign. Cost stated: a fills' net that OVER-counts (the D1 chain + s1 collision) is bounded by the venue's figure, never the other way. NOT done: the standing book's later increases under a persisting per-market disagreement (lane 34's territory); the refusal row's columns (no migration); the live proof of the preset.

Pinned (`backend/tests/test_e19_smaller_reading.py`): the rule pure (both signs, the shorts, disagreeing signs, zeros, non-numbers); admission with and without the flag / the fresh read / a bad number / a truthy stand-in; Martinez's shape opening at 1,197 with the plan's block and the placement at his cent; disagreeing signs opening nothing; the walk never sizing it; the smaller rounding to nothing; two readings within the max on the landed path; the short mirror image (-300 against -800); E12 at his level (1,197, `open_catchup`) and past the tolerance (197, `open_flow_only`); lane 34's section byte for byte and the standing book refused `drift`; Kostyuk collapsed to the share, the identical pair collapsed; Martinez's verbatim rows to 29,054.9 (the old key's four rows / 18,374.5 read beside); three price levels under one tx; the s1/poll pair identical to the cent; 0.001 sh / 0.09 % collapsed, 1 sh on 100 and 5,225 / 5,245 not; every reader through `his_fills`; the preset text and its case order; the census place, the docs, no knob.

The review's fold (2026-09-08, the same day): the OPENING tick clamps its sizing axis to `rules.smaller_reading(net_sized, the flag's net)` right before `mirror_target` (0 when the signs part), so a book admitted on the smaller reading never places on the larger one -- fills 30,000 against the venue's 25,104.1 opens 2,510, not 3,000; the short face -300 against -100 opens -100 -- with the $2,500 game cap still on top; the arm and the clamp both live in `_tick_book` on the in-memory flag alone (lane 34's drift section byte-identical; accepted by the orchestrator, lane 34 lands first). In `his_fills` the split arm measures the per-match rows against ONE source's net-leg rows (chain and s1 on one key stay D1's admitted collision, 30,328, the splits collapsed), the repeat arm collapses ONE per-match row per net-leg row (a ranked pairing: two makers of one size in one tx read 10,000, not 5,000), and both arms judge price as the same cent by round(., 2) on both sides or within half a cent in numeric to the mil (0.615 against 0.61 is one fill; a cent off stays two); the preset's NEW key carries the same rule.
