## 42. L7 (2026-09-08) — coverage: the tennis witness read, the exact lane's 404 trail named, the event-stale memo

Owner (~11:50Z, the P&L program): "Fix all 3 of these immediately ... This needs to be right." Coverage's NOT-mirrored $1,304,282/24 h (hard2/PNL_lane3_coverage.md, coverage_gap_1157, cand_refusals_1158) split into uptime ($463k: the first verdict after his last fill), venue-does-not-list ($237,888, certified by the venue's own rows through `resolve_explain`'s splits) and one UNPROVEN bucket: tennis under premap's bare `no_key_intersection` ($154,140/6 h; atp-gea-zandsch $98,210 / $200,546 over 24 h; wta-jones-serban $18,754; wta-carle-feistel $12,027; atp-binda-tunglin $9,350). The mirror's tennis lane asks the venue exactly two slug orders (`copy_sports._tennis_candidates`, `map_lane.exact_lane`), a 404 "never earns a refusal name" (§7.6), so the census could not say whether the venue was asked at all, and whether it LISTS those matches is not on disk. **ITEM 1 — THE READ FIRST.** A render-ops preset `tennis-witness=<YYYY-MM-DD>[:<surname>,<surname>...]` (the `book=<id>` pattern shape; the date is his slug's own, the surnames his event title's, lower-case letters only — the regex refuses anything else, so no name reaches SQL unchecked): the venue's aec- rows on that date whose `event_title` names any surname, then the date's aec row count per tour so 'no rows' can be told from 'no table rows that day'. Rows → the title witness (item 2: one aec row on his date naming BOTH surnames, the venue's own words, `tennis_title_absent` / `tennis_title_ambiguous` / `tennis_title_short`, behind a PREMAP_YN_IDENTITY-style switch) is built on them; no rows → 'venue does not list', the question closes. Item 2 is NOT built here: the read runs only through the landed workflow. **ITEM 3 — THE 404 TRAIL NAMED.** `_map_exact` counts the DISTINCT candidate slugs the exact lane asked and the venue answered 404 (collected in `_exact_for_token` as each one-slug ask is answered — the resolver's own '404' note, or the `_slug_404_until` memo's remembered answer for a slug it did not re-ask — each slug once however many of his tokens asked it), remembers the slugs and the count with the verdicts in `_map_cache` (a cache hit keeps the trail) and hands the count out as `out["exact_404"]`; `explain_unmapped(..., exact_404=n)` prints `<premap step>:exact:404:<n>` ONLY when premap's step carries no split (its keys never met the venue's rows: the venue's rows certified nothing) — on atp-gea-zandsch's shape `no_key_intersection:exact:404:5` reads "the two aec slug orders, the atc slug, `aec-<his slug>` and his own slug were asked, the venue listed none" (the fold of review MEDIUM-2: the count was every ask across both tokens, `:exact:404:12` for those five slugs). A split premap named from the venue's rows (`:venue:league-unlisted`, `:kind-absent`, `:line-absent`, ...) is the venue's word and stands byte for byte; the trail then lives on the row's `exact_404` detail alone. A refusal the lane named wins over both, as before; 0 or an unreadable count prints premap's name alone. `atp-binda-tunglin` ("Tung-Lin Wu": last token under three letters, zero candidates, no read) stays bare — the lane asked nothing, and saying more would be a guess. **ITEM 4 — THE EVENT-STALE MEMO** (the read cap is a rail: VENUE_CALLS_PER_TICK is capped_env, raising it is D4). `_tick_candidate` judges, right after his fills are read and before any map or quote read, `_event_stale`: the market's own date (the YYYY-MM-DD his slug names, `premap.date_of` — the market's identity, never a title) MORE THAN ONE DAY past (now >= its UTC day + 2 days: the whole day after it has run) AND his newest fill on it (the NEWEST of the walk's stamp, E7's `last_ts`, and every readable `ts` among the rows read — a fill of his ingested between the walk's query and this read sits in the rows, newer than the stamp, and is the witness; a row with no readable instant is no witness whatever the stamp says: read — the fold of review MEDIUM-1) older than `EVENT_STALE_FILL_S` (86,400 s) → `event_stale`: the D1 terminal memo (`_terminal_until`, `ms.UNMAPPED_TTL_S`) written by `_memo_event_stale` with the read's `at` beside it in `_event_stale_memo`, the candidate skipped `cand_terminal_skipped` until the TTL runs OR his next fill lands (a walk stamp newer than `at`) OR the market wakes — released at the walk by `_release_cand_memo` as the E7 memos are, `cand_memo_released`, the market read that tick (the fold of review HIGH-1: the memo is the ABSENCE of his fills, which his next fill undoes at once; D1's own entries carry no `at` and stay TTL-bound — the venue's terminal word, which his fill cannot undo; the sibling is not persisted, so after a restart the reloaded entry stays TTL-bound too, at most 900 s), a refusal row carrying the NAME alone (us_slug, mark, his_net NULL — the row's fixed columns have no place for `event_date` / `fill_age_s`, which ride the candidate's ctx only; the fold of review LOW-1), NO venue call. Either witness missing — an undated slug, a date the calendar refuses, no readable fill instant, a fill inside the day, a wake this tick — reads as today (fail closed toward the read the rail already bounds). Both windows are constants, not knobs (`EVENT_STALE_DAY_S`, `EVENT_STALE_FILL_S`): a knob could only make the memo fire on a live market. The one new census key, `event_stale`, sits before E13's `venue_market_ended` (every pinned tail stands). ## 43. E19b (2026-09-08) — the collapse key withdrawn from sizing: fills-vs-venue's first run

THE FIRST RUN (`fills-vs-venue`, 17:07Z, ten minutes after e9cda86 deployed at 16:57Z; hard2/post_fvv_1707.txt). 359 markets he traded in 24 h with a book, 83 with a venue figure: the summed gap to the venue's net 81,470.2 sh under the OLD key (D1, section 8) against 119,856.5 sh under the NEW key (lane 8's, section 41); the old key closer on 22 markets (old_closer 22), the new closer on 4 (new_closer 4); raw 8,683,766.1 sh, 3,313,258.2 dropped under the old key, 786,112.1 under the new, $1,267,599 the new key kept. On LIVE books with no fill after the plan's clock (later_sh 0.0) the OLD key reads the venue to the decimal and the NEW key over-reads by thousands of shares: book 611 aec-cs2-g2-ast-2026-09-08 (live) old_net -966.9 = snap_net -966.9, new_net +19,349.5 (chain 301 rows / 113,737.2 sh, s1 5 / 1,398.3, poll 72 / 67,966.3; 66,580.3 sh dropped under the old key, 15,780.3 under the new; the plan's drift 1.049972; gap_old 0.0, gap_new 20,316.4); 601 aec-cs2-gl-furia-2026-09-08 (closed) old_net 2,228.3 = snap_net 2,228.3, new_net 12,355.8 (chain 78 / 25,611.7, poll 9 / 18,534.2; gap_old 0.0, gap_new 10,127.5); 622 atc-spl-haz-taa-2026-09-08-taa (live) old 15,000 = venue 15,000, new 20,000 (chain 3 rows / 15,000 sh, poll ONE row / 5,000; drift 0.25; gap_new 5,000.0); 656 atc-ucl-bru-ast-2026-09-08-bru (live) old -9,150.7 = venue -9,150.7, new -13,746.8 (chain 24 / 9,474.5, poll 4 / 4,596.1; drift 0.334339; gap_new 4,596.1). The mirror_live heartbeat at 17:04:48Z read census drift 7 where the 16:25:16Z read had drift 0 — drift 0 -> 7 across the deploy (hard2/tick_1705.txt against h1625.txt: 611's row drift 1.113167 / his_net 18,250.96 / target 157 against 16:25Z's drift 0 / 11,470.47 / 1,147; 613 drift 0.811729 / his_net 2,540.98 against 0 / 546.39; 633 drift 0.506053 / -154.0 against 0 / -74.51).

WHAT THE ROWS SAY. The new key counts a per-match row under a chain key unless the per-match rows sum to ONE source's net-leg rows within max(0.01 sh, 0.1 %) at the same price to half a cent, or the row repeats a net-leg row at the same cent and size. On these books the poll lane's rows under a chain key are a PARTIAL set of the maker matches the chain row already holds (622: one 5,000 row under 15,000 of chain legs; 656: four rows / 4,596.1 under 24 chain rows / 9,474.5 — all of them dropped by the old key, none by the new), so they neither sum to the leg nor repeat it, and the new key counted them ON TOP of the chain row that the venue's own per-market figure says already held them. An over-read of his net inflates the mirror's target (10 % of his net); the drift gate refused those increases (fail closed — the census's 7), but a reader that sizes on MORE of his flow than the venue holds cannot stand: when in doubt the mirror sizes on LESS.

THE WITHDRAWAL. `mirror_shadow.his_fills` is 7a4b852's text byte for byte — D1's key: ONE reading per (whale, tx, asset, side), the net-leg row when the key holds one, else the per-match rows — and it is THE reader for every sizing path: the candidate, the book, the shadow's `his_paired_sh` / `fills_dedup_*`, the report (lane 8 replaced the function, not its callers; the function is replaced back, and the callers are pinned unchanged). Lane 8's key is kept in the module under its own name, `his_fills_distinct`, with its own counter `his_fills_distinct_dedup`, so a call never moves what `his_fills_dedup` reports to the census; NO money path calls it (pinned: the live worker, the report, the shadow's market read and its tick never name it; the module's one occurrence of `his_fills_distinct(` is the def). Its docstring carries the first run's numbers — why it is not wired — and lane 8's rule verbatim, for the record; it is the reference the preset's `new_*` columns compute (the same SQL) and the figure a redesign is measured against. Part (a) is UNTOUCHED: `rules.smaller_reading`, `AdmissionFacts.drift_sized_smaller`, the census name `drift_smaller_open`, the opening tick's clamp — the Martinez shape (fills 11,974.6 against the venue's 25,104, both long) still opens 1,197 at ratio 0.1 on the SMALLER reading. Part (c), the fills-vs-venue preset, is UNTOUCHED (its OLD column is the reader again, its NEW column the reference). No knob, no migration, no census name added or moved; the loss stop, the rails and the switches untouched.

THE COST STATED. D1's key under-reads Martinez's long (15,925.4 for the venue's 29,054.9; the net 11,974.5 at 12:10:43Z's rows): on the fills' side the reopen reads low again, and part (a) covers it from the venue's side (the fresh per-market read, the smaller of the two, which is what the owner's question needed). D1's residuals stand as D1 pinned them (test_d1_review): the chain + s1 collision on one key is an over-read the rule admits; the s1 same-asset deferral under-reads a SELL sweep's second leg (his position reads larger than the venue's). The measure says D1 is the closer reader on the day; neither key is the venue.

THE PINS RE-PINNED, each saying why. test_e19_smaller_reading (the (b) block): Kostyuk's shape (both keys agree — read on both); Martinez's 29,054.9 on the reference alone, THE READER at 15,925.4 / 9,175.9 with 4 rows / 18,374.5 sh dropped and the net 11,974.5; three price levels (reference all three; reader 5,225 with 8,846 dropped); the identical s1/poll pair and the cent witness (reference; reader the three s1 rows, 5,445 dropped); the dust (reference; reader 10,200 / 5,225, four rows); every reader through `his_fills` with D1's one clause, the reference's arms named on `his_fills_distinct`, no money path naming it; the docs pin reads this section. test_pnl_l8_review_pins: MEDIUM-1's twins (reference 10,000 / 25,000; reader 5,000 / 15,000 with 10,000 / 25,000 sh dropped); LOW-1 and LOW-2 (both keys agree — read on both); CRITICAL-1, HIGH-1, the preset on the real migrations and the help line untouched. The D1 pins go back to 7a4b852's expectations — "a chain row wins even when the poll legs do not sum to it" (15,114 / 270, two rows 5,196 dropped), the randomised "never over max(chain, poll)", the s1 deferral's under-read (5,000 against the venue's 2,000 on a SELL, 5,000 against 8,000 on a BUY), the five SQL mutants on D1's clauses — and lane 8's rewrites of them stand beside as pins of the withdrawn behaviour on the unwired function (`test_e19b_reference_*`: the E19 rule in Python on the same seed, the deferral's venue figure, the seven mutants on the two arms); the "FROM trades t" count in the shadow reads 4 (his_fills, compute_ratio, active_conditions, the unwired reference), with the callers named. The E9 review's Q9 pin reads D1's clause again. Every census name and every emit test unchanged (test_mirror_live_worker, test_e9_review_pins green).

WHAT REMAINS. A redesign of the collapse judged by fills-vs-venue — the OLD column must equal the venue where it does today and the NEW column must beat it on the day's totals (gap and closer-count) before anything is wired — with books 611 / 622 / 656 / 601's RAW rows (their chain / s1 / poll rows under each tx key, beside the plan's mkt_long / mkt_other / snap_net) as fixtures on the scratch database the D1 file builds, Martinez's rows beside them, and D1's two residuals in the same fixture set; the ingest-side dedupe on (tx, asset, side) with a sum check that D1's docstring names as the durable fix; a second fills-vs-venue read after this lands (the OLD column's gap should be the day's whole gap again; the NEW column stays as the reference's measure).

## 44. E20 (2026-09-08) — the desk-wide wrong-sign trip is the genuine inversion's alone; a co-hold of the other sign freezes the one book

THE INCIDENT (mirror-state 21:31Z; hard2/book_663_2132.txt; owner 21:27Z: "Trades are not firing correctly"). Book 663 (atc-ucl-bvb-vil-2026-09-08-bvb, a SHORT of 1,011 shares at 0.6523 average: 3,351.1 sold across 12 fills, 2,340 covered across 4, the ledger −1,012 at the trip's read) read +69 from the venue's positions walk from 20:44:58Z through 21:08:03Z (the shadow's rows) — exactly the size of the last 69-share cover (order 4575, 20:37:44Z). The SHADOW judged the book `frozen: venue and ledger disagree` from 20:44:58Z (its rows); the live row's `frozen_reason` is `wrong_sign_trip`, which `_SQL_BOOK_FREEZE` writes only on a book not yet frozen (the first reason sticks), so the live book was LIVE at 20:57:19Z and the trip was its first freeze. At 20:57:19Z the wrong-sign branch of step M — `r.venue != 0 and ledger != 0 and (r.venue < 0) != (ledger < 0)` on ONE read — wrote `mirror_live` false with the receipt `{why: wrong_sign_trip, book: 663, venue: 69, ledger: −1012, manual: 0, registered: 0}`, cancelled every open order and put the desk in exits-only mode: the last order row 20:53:48Z (order 4621, a reduce), `books_live` 4 and `books_frozen` 0 on the 21:27:41Z heartbeat (`mode` "exits", `mode_db_off` 5; tick_2127.txt), 123 fills of his ingested in the 21Z hour, 6 on our markets and 0 answered within 30 min (hourly_2131.txt, the by-hour rows) — 34 minutes off and counting at the 21:31Z mirror-state. The reading could not be the inversion the branch guards against — our leg booked as the OTHER side, whose venue magnitude is the leg's — because 69 is not 1,011. The market expired and the book settled +469.11 at 21:20:16Z (his −6,355.63 on the market: the short was the right side).

THE RULE. In the wrong-sign branch, `genuine = abs(abs(r.venue) − abs(ledger)) <= mi.VENUE_LEDGER_TOL_SHARES` (1.0 share). GENUINE: the desk-wide trip on ONE read exactly as before — `_trip_live_off("wrong_sign_trip", …)` with its receipt, the book's open orders cancelled under the name, the freeze under `wrong_sign_trip`, and on a short book the short proof's negative verdict (`le._record_short_proof(ok=False)`, `plan.short_proof = mismatch`) — an inversion is not a lag, and a one-tick delay on it would leave every short at risk. NOT GENUINE: a co-hold the ledger cannot explain — THIS book freezes under `wrong_sign_hold` (`_freeze`: the `frozen_reasons` gauge, the recent row and the once-per-transition census count are the freeze's own; on a book already frozen under another name — the first reason sticks on the row — the hold is counted and listed once, on the transition, and the same reading next tick moves the gauge and the tick count only), its open orders are cancelled under that name, `plan.wrong_sign_hold = {venue, ledger, manual, registered, at}` carries the reading, and the desk keeps trading: no receipt, `mirror_live` untouched, no short-proof verdict (the tally the short gates read is the genuine inversion's alone). NO frozen exit: `FROZEN_EXIT_REASONS` names `placement_lost` and `venue_ledger_disagree` alone, so `_frozen_exit` refuses `reason_not_eligible` under the hold exactly as under `wrong_sign_trip` — the venue's own position is the one figure the hold cannot read, and a reading of the other sign on a short would refuse `frozen_venue_flat` in any case. `wrong_sign_hold` joins `_VENUE_MAY_HOLD_REASONS` (E13: a frozen book under it is never ended on the venue's terminal word while the venue may hold shares the ledger does not). THE THAW: the hold is the same walk-instability class as `venue_ledger_disagree` (the same unstable per-market read as book 595), so it thaws under D2's two-reads rule beside it (`_TWO_READS_THAW_REASONS`; `ml-book-thaw-agrees` names both): two consecutive FRESH agreeing reads thaw it, one read is `thaw_held: one_read`, a cached read `cached_read`, the D2 switch off `thaw_off`. E5's one-read thaw — which a `wrong_sign_trip` freeze kept, with the desk off behind it — would have placed the live plan on the first agreeing read of a flickering walk (the review's probe: ledger 10 held on −5, thawed and placed 290 on the next read of 10). A flat ledger still thaws on one read (nothing resumes; the flat book closes). Fail closed on the book, never on the desk for a reading that is not the inversion.

THE NAMES. Census `wrong_sign_hold`, before `drift_smaller_open`: keys[−15] once E14b (section 46) placed `exit_take_rested` at keys[−14], with `drift_smaller_open` keys[−13] and `registered_no_increase` keys[−12] as before; the from-end pins the insertion moves — test_e17_standing_reanchor (−21:−13 → −22:−14), test_e9_review_pins (−89:−85 → −90:−86, −90 → −91), test_mirror_live_worker (−89: → −90:, −90 → −91) — moved by one; every pin at −13 and nearer the tail stands. Plan `wrong_sign_hold`. Frozen reason `wrong_sign_hold`. No rail, no knob, no migration, no decision word, no change to `wrong_sign_trip`'s receipt or to the P2 integrity block (`_INTEG_CENSUS_KEYS` is pinned to its committed text). The census key sits past the served cap of 40 like `ledger_dust` and does not ride on `integ`, so an operator reads the hold off the heartbeat's `frozen_reasons` gauge and `frozen-detail`, never off the served census.

THE PINS (backend/tests/test_e20_wrong_sign_hold.py, 13 functions / 15 cases): ledger 10 read −5 → the hold, the desk untouched, the order cancelled, nothing placed, the plan's reading; 663's shape (a short of 1,011 at 0.6523 read +69) → the hold, no trip, no short-proof verdict; the mid-tick shape of the trip pin with a hold instead → the other book's rest kept, the candidate opens and places; a re-freeze under the hold counts nothing (V3-2); a book frozen `venue_ledger_disagree` first (663 as the shadow told it) counts and lists the hold once and the gauge each tick; the hold thaws on the second fresh agreeing read, never the first (`thaw_held: one_read`, nothing placed); a held book never follows his exit (`reason_not_eligible`, nothing placed on either sign of read); the genuine inversion at −10 / −11 / −9 (the tolerance's edges) → the trip byte for byte, and −12 → the hold; a short of 300 read +300 → the trip with the short proof's verdict; a same-sign read past the tolerance → E16's suspect, neither arm; the census place, the may-hold list, the source shape (the magnitude test guards the trip; the verdict is written only on the genuine arm); the docs. Re-pinned, each saying why: test_e16_freeze_two_reads (the one-read trip now on the genuine inversion, −5 → −10; the thaw statement names the two reasons), test_mirror_live_worker (the trip receipt and the mid-tick trip, −5 → −10; the sign-proof pin's co-hold block, +500 against −300 → the hold; the tail indexes), test_e9_review_pins (the tail indexes), test_e13_review_pins (the may-hold names).

LIVE PROOF. `mirror-state`: no `mirror_live_trip` written on a co-hold; `frozen-detail` / the heartbeat's `frozen_reasons` naming `wrong_sign_hold` on the next 663-shaped read while `mode` stays "on". NOT DONE HERE: why the venue's positions walk reported +69 for a short of 1,011 for 24 minutes on an OPEN market (the same unstable per-market read as book 595, task 73) — a venue question, read next; the re-arm itself is the operator's `mirror-on` and is never automatic.

## 45. The side-band rail and the measurement presets (2026-09-08, FILL lane 0)

THE RULE, FIRST HALF (lane 0a, the one rail the environment could raise). The candidate's side band -- how far the ask may sit from his price at the open before the admission clause refuses `side_band` -- was a bare environment read in the live worker (`band = rules._env_float("LIVE_SIDE_PRICE_BAND"); band = 0.15 if band is None else band`, mirror_live.py 11020-11021 at ab2525c), so a value above 0.15 in the environment WIDENED the open's price band: the one rail on the money path a shell could raise (FILL_plan line 29). Now `rules.LIVE_SIDE_PRICE_BAND_MAX = capped_env("LIVE_SIDE_PRICE_BAND", 0.15, floor=0.0)` sits beside `MIRROR_CATCHUP_MAX_CENTS` (the rail comment names the copy lane's executor, which reads the same variable at its own side check and is not this rail's caller) and `_tick_candidate` reads `band = float(rules.LIVE_SIDE_PRICE_BAND_MAX)`; `d["band"]` is unchanged, so the 054 refusal row's `band` column keeps printing the band the tick admitted on. No behaviour changes at the default. The rail: capped_env 0.15 floor 0.0 (rules 236-261: honoured within [floor, default]; unreadable or non-finite lands on the default; 0 admits only an ask AT his price). Census / plan / decision: none new; the refusal name `side_band` and the 054 row's `band` column as before. Fail closed: junk, empty, inf, nan or a raise in the environment -> 0.15; a negative -> 0.0 (the most closed setting: nothing opens away from his price). Tests: test_fill_0a_side_band_rail.py 7 -- the constant and its call site pinned in source (the bare `_env_float` / `os.getenv` reads gone from the worker), the env sweep (0.10 honoured, 0.30 -> 0.15, 2.0 -> 0.15, junk / inf / nan / empty -> 0.15, -1 -> 0.0), the constant's OWN import-time read in a fresh interpreter under the environment (0.10 -> 0.10, 0.30 -> 0.15, junk -> 0.15, -1 -> 0.0, unset -> 0.15; the review's MEDIUM-1: the behavioural pins set the constant by name, so a floor equal to the default or a swapped variable name passed every one of them and died only on a source string), env 0.10 refusing an ask 0.12 over his price that the default admits (the 054 row's band 0.10), env 0.30 still refusing an ask 0.20 over, and Martinez 534's shape (task 71: his 0.61, the market 0.81 / 0.82, `_martinez` from test_e19_smaller_reading) refused `side_band` at the default with the row's band 0.15, ask 0.82, his_px 0.61. test_e19_smaller_reading's rail count re-pinned 20 -> 21 (the 21st capped_env). Live proof: `cand-refusals` (render-ops.yml, the `band` column of the 054 rows) never above 0.15 on a `side_band` row after the deploy. Does not fix: the side-band refusals themselves (the Martinez reopen), which are an admission question the `closed-while-he-traded` preset below names per book.

THE RULE, SECOND HALF (lane 0b, measurement first: the reads every later lane is judged by, on today's rows). Six read-only presets or preset changes in render-ops.yml, nothing in the worker, no census name, no plan field, no decision word written (059's list, restated unchanged: the placement words 'rest', 'take', 'cover', 'exit_rest' with 'take_in_band' reserved, and the cause words a replace or a TTL cancel writes OVER the cancelled row's `decision` -- 'replace_cent', 'replace_qty', 'replace_side', 'ttl', 'replace_unread' (059's comment; E18's `_SQL_ORDER_DECISION`) -- READ by take-band, exits-band and fills-missed's (class, decision) block): (1) `take-band`, new, wired into the hourly beside fills-missed (nine presets since; it was eight since lane M): every entry order row of the last 24 h on a LONG book (`o.kind IN ('increase', 'take')`, `o.side = 'BUY_LONG'` -- the row's plan-side value, book_347_1750 398 and the exits-paired clause; the plan's `o.side = 'BUY'` is that value's shorthand -- `b.intent = 'ORDER_INTENT_BUY_LONG'`) graded at OUR price (the wire) against the long token's resolved price, per (`decision`, band bucket) with band_c = (ask_at_place - floor-cent of his_level) x 100 on the numeric-rounded cent (lane M's HIGH-3) bucketed <=0 / 1 / 2 / 3+ / unread: n, filled_n, fill_pct, our_usd, n_resolved, roi, ci95, paid_med_c = the median of (wire - floor-cent of his_level) x 100 over the filled rows, send_ask_over_wire_med_c over IOC rows; 'unrecorded' for a row older than 059; a replaced or expired entry rest reads 'rest' -- the only placement word a replace cancels on a long book's BUY_LONG row -- with `replaced_n` beside (the same fold as (2)'s: without it the 'rest' row's fill_pct counted only the rests that were never moved); ROLLUP rows read ALL. It is brief question 3's read (hourly_1737 1746-1747 today: increase 1,922 rows, 1,827 with a band, 661 filled, 16.0 % at or through, 42.2 % inside 1c, 60.4 % inside 2c, band median 2.0c, fill 42.2 % inside 1c against 31.5 % outside; take 557 / 364 filled / 65.4 %) split by the word that placed the row. (2) `exits-band`, new, standalone: every reducing-side order row of the last 24 h (the exits-paired `reducing` clause) with the row's PLACEMENT word -- 'exit_rest' / 'take' / 'cover' (lane 3's 'exit_take_in_band' / 'cover_in_band', unwritten today) -- where a row a replace cancelled carries the cause instead (059: 'replace_cent' / 'replace_qty' / 'replace_side' / 'ttl' / 'replace_unread', written over `decision` by E18's `_SQL_ORDER_DECISION` at the replace and the TTL cancel), so the cause words are in the population, read as 'exit_rest' on a long book and 'cover' on a short one (rules.order_decision: a reducing rest is 'exit_rest', a short book's buy-back 'cover'; an IOC is never the open row a replace cancels), with `replaced_n` beside (the review's HIGH-1: without the fold the held-then-moved rests -- the read's subject -- were outside the set, and the rows left under 'exit_rest' were the ones that filled, are still open, or were cancelled for another reason), band_c = (his_level - bid_at_place) x 100 on a SELL_LONG and (ask_at_place - his_level) x 100 on a cover, bucketed <=0 / 1 / 2 / 3-5 / >5 / unread; per (decision, bucket) n, filled_any, filled_whole, shares_unfilled, usd_unfilled, done_med_s; then per (decision, bucket) the BOOKS whose rows filled anything against those whose never did with mirror_books.settled_pnl summed on each side (settled_when_filled / settled_when_unfilled) -- R5's unread A8 on today's rows, the only read that can price a second cent (owner decision D2) before it is paid. Follow-on, not built: the row records the re-read's ask only (059 `ask_at_send`); a SELL IOC's send-time bid lives on the plan. (3) `exits-paired` gains `trough` (his lowest running net after the peak; `min(hr.net_after)` over the after-peak rows, the plan's FILTER on hn written on hr, which already is that set), `his_reduced_pct = 1 - trough / peak`, and the verdict `he_reduced_we_held` -- `hs.exit_ts IS NULL` (no exit by the 10 % rule), his_reduced_pct >= 0.25 and under half our peak filled after his first reducing fill (0.25 / 0.5 chosen) -- placed BEFORE `no_exit_by_him` in the CASE (the plan's "after" is unreachable in a CASE that reads in order; the arm is that verdict's own sub-case), counted in stuck_after_his_exit_usd and in the totals as reduced_we_held / reduced_we_held_stuck_usd; test_render_ops_exits_paired's VERDICTS gains the word in that place. Book 266 (hourly_1737 1424: his peak 30,993.0, our 1,955.0 sh, staked 977.65, settled -977.65; he cut 30 %) reads `he_reduced_we_held` on the 266-shaped scratch fixture. (4) `fills-missed` gains a `state` column from the book windows -- in_book (a window, opened_at - 120 s to closed_at, holds the fill), before_open (older than the earliest window), after_close (at or after the newest close with no book live), no_book (between two books) -- as a second statement grouped by (class, state) beside the ALL rollup, and the answering row's `decision` with its band_c on the per-fill rows for a third statement over filled / partial / missed_expired_ioc grouped by (class, decision); six statements now (the classes, state, decision, per book, the band table, by hour), one chain shared by the first four; the order's own state rides `o` as `ostate` beside the window's `state` (the review's CRITICAL-1: `o` carries `s.*`, which holds the window column, so a bare `ord.state` beside it made every `o.state` in `g` ambiguous on the real schema -- the whole chain refused, and with it the hourly, which the runner sends as one string under ON_ERROR_STOP; pglast parses an ambiguous reference, only the catalog refuses it); the (class, decision) block prints a cause word where the answering rest was replaced or expired (its class is 'missed_replace' or 'partial'; the word is left as written, so the record and the read agree). Today it splits `unseen` 1,105 fills / $517,203.44 (hourly_1737 1660; ALL 3,234, 1659) by a GROUP BY instead of an argument. (5) `closed-while-he-traded`, new, standalone: per book closed in the last 24 h by the mirror's OWN close -- `last_plan->>'close' IN ('cashed_out', 'cancelled')` OR `last_reason IN ('closed_cashed_out', 'closed_cancelled')`, because the same tick's plan write can overwrite the close's reason (post_booksnew_1707 385: book 309 last_reason `on target`, plan close `cashed_out`, net -16,589.9; row 380: 304 `closed_cancelled`); never the settle's `closed: standing row *` nor `venue_market_ended` -- opened / closed, the plan's close / sign_flip / turn / reopen_refused (the last two lane 5's, NULL before it), his net at the close, his fills AFTER closed_at on the condition (48 h rows collapsed chain-first: n, usd, shares, his P&L at his price on resolved markets, his last fill), his net over the 48 h rows on the book's axis (the `r` CTE's window, not a whole-position figure), the next book (id, opened, lag_s, state, settled), the reopen path from mirror_candidate_refusals after the close (refusals, the first name, name@HH:MM ... left 200, the cand-refusals idiom); the totals (books, with_later_fills, later_usd, later_sh, later_pnl_at_his_px, reopened_n, refused_only_n, no_verdict_n, sign_flips) and the books by first refusal name. Today it must print 309 / 338 / 630 with 151,699.7 / 57,113.2 / 52,996.0 later shares (post_fvv_1707 332 / 338 / 340; 338's net 12,468.2 -> -1,715.8 -> -20,300.5, verify_1750 359) and the reopen path each took, which no file holds. (6) the target line (owner decision D3): `paired-day` and `paired-ratio` pair PER EPISODE -- a market pairs on any settled episode, our stake and settled figure summed over the closed books with settled_pnl, `episodes` / `settled_eps` printed beside (before, one live or unsettled episode excluded the market whole: 58 mixed markets holding +4,276.04 of our settled winners, FILL_plan line 46) -- drop the zero-stake rows from the totals' dollar sums and ratios (every `sum(...)` in both totals runs `FILTER (WHERE COALESCE(our_staked, 0) > 0)`; zero_stake_mkts and zero_stake_settled beside; sign_agree still counts every row so an OPPOSITE book stays named: book 253, hourly_1737 1425, our_staked 0.00 / settled +735.41 credited to the set today), and the hourly's one line leads with the net ratio: `PAIRED <n> mkts (<staked> staked, <k> zero-stake dropped) his_roi_net .. our_roi .. roi_ratio_net ..x gross his_roi .. roi_ratio ..x same a/b opposite ...` (today's whole set, hourly_1737 1487: 114 markets, his_roi -0.0622 gross / -0.1562 net, ours -0.2821, roi_ratio 4.54x gross / 1.81x net). The pattern arms `paired-day=<YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM[:SS]Z>` and `paired-ratio=<...>` are the bare presets' own SQL with `b.opened_at >= that clock` on every books window (2 on paired-day, 4 on paired-ratio; the arm's regex admits nothing but that shape), so section 3's read -- the ratio on books opened after a deploy -- is one dispatch; the bare labels and the hourly stay the whole set. The help arm lists every label (take-band, exits-band, closed-while-he-traded after exits-paired, hourly last) and every pattern in case order (book, paired-day, paired-ratio, tennis-witness). Every SQL string parses under pglast; the hourly's output today ran 1,074 lines (hourly_1737 772-1846) under the runner's 1,500 cap, and the three blocks this lane adds to it are bucketed rows only.

THE TESTS (0b). test_render_ops_take_band.py 5, test_render_ops_exits_band.py 5, test_render_ops_closed_while_he_traded.py 5, test_render_ops_fills_missed.py 5, test_render_ops_paired_ratio.py 5, test_render_ops_exits_paired.py +1 (each on the exits-paired model: the case label present and ordered, hourly last, the help arm regenerated, the statements and their measures pinned as words, read-only; fills-missed's word pin also reads `ord.state AS ostate`, `o.ostate = 'cancelled'` and exactly one `ord.state` in the chain, the review's CRITICAL-1 without a database; exits-band's fifth is the review's scratch-database pin on the held shape -- a long book with a replaced exit rest, an expired one and an open one, each 2c past his price at the touch, beside a short book's filled cover: ALL n 4, exit_rest / 2 n 3 with replaced_n 2, shares_unfilled 300.0, usd_unfilled 163.0 = 100 x 0.55 + 100 x 0.54 + 100 x 0.54, cover / 2 filled_whole 1, no row under a cause word, and the books statement settled_when_unfilled 50.0 against settled_when_filled -40.0; without the fold the set read n 2); test_render_ops_hourly.py's eight-preset pin is the nine-preset pin (PARTS gains take-band after fills-missed); the scratch-database pins (skipped without a local server, as the 69 real-SQL pins are) read book 347's shape (three books on one condition, his fills before the first open / inside a window / between two books / after the last close -> before_open / in_book / no_book / after_close; a take row filled whole and a rest row filled whole -> filled / take at band 1c and filled / rest at 2c), book 266's shape (he_reduced_we_held with his_reduced_pct 0.30 and stuck 977.65, beside two controls: he cut 30 % and we sold 60 % -> no_exit_by_him; he cut 10 % -> no_exit_by_him) and book 309's shape (plan close cashed_out under an overwritten last_reason, two later fills with his P&L at his price, the reopen path `drift@.. side_band@..`, no next book; a settle-closed book never in the set). ## 46. E14b (2026-09-08, FILL lane 1)

THE RULE. A long book's exit keeps E4's prices exactly (section 13): ONE IOC at the take cent — the lowest cent at or above his exit price less `rules.MIRROR_EXIT_TOL` (0.01, `capped_env`, floor 0.0: the environment may only tighten it) — whenever the bid is at or through it, the rest post-only at ceil(his), nothing chasing outside the cent. What changes: when that IOC is WITHHELD at the send (E18's re-read, section 36: `bid_moved`, `ioc_quote_unread`) or FILLS ONLY PART of its quantity, the unfilled quantity RESTS at his cent on the SAME tick — exactly as the entry's `_entry_take` already rests its remainder — instead of leaving the book with no exit order until the next full tick planned again (`POLL_S` 30 s). Before this lane the keep branch cancelled the standing rest and sent the IOC whose re-read could withhold it, and the no-rest exit's "partial fill leaves NOTHING resting" was the rule by name (E4). The rows (hard2/post_exits_1707.txt, 17:07Z): of 354 books closed in 24 h he cashed out on 93; we exited with him on 34, partially on 23, placed an exit that never filled on 10 and placed none on 10; stuck_usd 1,190.43; lag median 75 s, p90 1,043 s; cents_vs_his median 0.4c (row 396). The shapes this lane is built on: book 334 cfb-smu-flst spread, his exit 0.549, our 358, filled 0.50 at a lag of 279 s (row 335); book 467 atp-serna-jianu, his 0.250, our 570, filled 0.43 (row 333); book 419 itf-olivar-hashimo, his 0.180, filled 0.09 at a lag of 3 s (row 336); book 285 lal-elc-rso total, his 0.840, our exit average 0.718, filled 0.05 (row 334); book 661's exit rest 609 @0.620 partially filled 89.24 then cancelled `replace` (hard2/closerows_1750.txt row 529, order 3632). No file counts the exit IOCs withheld `bid_moved` or the partials whose remainder waited a tick, so nothing is claimed in dollars; the upper bound, not a forecast: the 23 `partial_exit` books' stuck loss in the under-50% class −595.06 plus the 50-80% class −133.33 = −728.39 (FILL_R3_exit_path.md 2.2), times the long flatten rows' all-time fill rate 105 / (182 + 15 + 105 + 3 + 6 = 311) = 33.8% (closerows_1750 rows 586-590) = at most $246.20 on the closed set, and less, because a bid back at his cent within the tick is taken by the next plan anyway. Strictly non-negative by construction: no price changes.

THE CODE. `mirror_live._exit_take(t, book, r, p, ex, qty, his_px, plan, kind)` beside `_entry_take`, its mirror image: `_place(..., "take", SELL, ex["take"], qty, ..., tif="IOC")`; then, only when the result is one of `IOC_SKIPPED` (nothing executed: the whole `qty` is the remainder) or `'take'`, and never on a tick that tripped (`t.cancel_all`) or abandoned, on a frozen book, or with a row of the book non-terminal (`t.nonterminal`) — `_entry_take`'s guards — the remainder `left = min(qty − floor(take_filled), _sell_qty(book, qty − floor(take_filled)))`: never more than the plan's own unfilled quantity (the IOC's booking read off the plan's `take_filled`, written by `_place_reserved` before the row is finished) AND never more than the ledger reads NOW (`_sell_qty`: the IOC's fill is booked into the ledger by `_book_delta` before `_finish_order` returns; a booking that failed froze the book and left the row non-terminal, which the guards refuse — the two judges' reviewer item, pinned). `left >= 1` → `_place(..., kind or "reduce", SELL, ex["rest"], left, ...)`. The two long-exit sites of `_act` route through it — the keep branch's take off a standing rest (the cancel first, as today) and the no-rest exit — and nowhere else: `rules.exit_terms`, `MIRROR_EXIT_TOL`, `_exit_held`, the short cover (`_cover_qty`, `_s4_refusal`, the S4 sites), `_flatten_send`'s co-held IOC, `_flatten_vanished`, `_frozen_exit` / `_frozen_reduce_on_fill`, the E12 ratchet, the E15 witness and the day stop are byte-identical. The next tick's take off the same-tick rest is today's keep branch (the bid inside the cent cancels the rest and sends the IOC).

THE NAMES. Census `exit_take_rested` (the same-tick rest placed after a withheld or partial exit IOC), inserted before E19's `drift_smaller_open` (keys[−13]) and `registered_no_increase` (keys[−12]); the tail pins that count from the end move by one (−89 → −90, −90 → −91; E17's eight −21:−13 → −22:−14). Plan `exit_take_rested` {take, rest, qty, filled, rested} whenever a rest was attempted (`rested` 0 when its placement was refused by name) — the plan's key is the census name, the file's convention (`bid_moved`, `exit_out_of_tol`, `kept_min_life`); the plan's four E4 cents `exit_px` / `exit_floor` / `exit_rest` / `exit_take` stand as they are (the cent `exit_take` is not overwritten). Decision: the IOC's row keeps `'take'`; the same-tick rest's row writes `'exit_rest'` (`rules.order_decision` on the reduce leg — an existing word at a new site). No new decision word: migration 059's list stays `'rest'`, `'take'`, `'cover'`, `'exit_rest'` (with `'take_in_band'` reserved).

THE RAILS. None added; no knob, no migration. `MIRROR_EXIT_TOL` untouched. An exit's ops are exempt from the ops budget (`_op_slot(exit=True)`, M-1) and the replace budget (`_exit_or_flip`), so the extra rest is never `ops_capped` and the next tick's cancel-to-take never `take_capped` (pinned at an ops budget of 0).

FAIL CLOSED. The IOC withheld `bid_moved` / `ioc_quote_unread` (or `ask_moved` / `ioc_reread_capped`, unreachable on an exit) → the whole quantity rests at ceil(his) this tick (before: nothing until the next tick). The tick tripped or abandoned, the book frozen, a row non-terminal → nothing more this tick, the IOC's result returned as today. The IOC filled the whole quantity → nothing to rest, `'take'` returned, nothing on the plan. A remainder of a share or more that `_sell_qty` reads none to sell (the ledger or the standing row under it) → `under_one_share` on the census and `exit_take_rested` {…, `rested` 0} on the plan, no rest sized past the ledger (the review's MEDIUM-1). The rest's INSERT refused by the one-open-order index → `open_order_pending` on the census, `rested` 0 on the plan; a remainder under `rules.MIRROR_MIN_ORDER_USD` (1.0, `capped_env`) at the rest cent on a reduce that is not a flatten → `under_min_notional`, `rested` 0 (a flatten is exempt, as every flatten rest is; the same refusal today's next-tick rest meets). The venue refusing the keep branch's cancel → `cancel_refused:<reason>` / `cancel_pending` as today, no IOC and no rest. No exit price of his (`exit_px_src: 'none'`) → E2's take-at-wire path, untouched. The bid outside the cent at the tick → `exit_out_of_tol`, the rest at his cent, no re-read, untouched. A frozen book's exit (E5 / E16) → its IOC as today and no rest. A short book's cover → S4's path, none of the new names.

THE TESTS (`backend/tests/test_e14b_exit_take_rests.py`, 15). Book 334's shape: bid 0.54 at the tick, the re-read 0.53 → `bid_moved` → the 358 rest at 0.55 the same tick, decision `exit_rest`, `exit_take_rested` 1, one op, no IOC row, never `ops_capped` at a budget of 0; the next tick holds it `exit_out_of_tol`. The unreadable re-read (`ioc_quote_unread`) rests the whole quantity too. Book 467's shape: the IOC for 570 fills 245 → the 325 rest at 0.25 the same tick (the ledger 895), decisions `take` then `exit_rest`; the next tick with the bid back cancels the rest and takes the 325. Book 419's shape: bid 0.16 under the floor 0.17 → no IOC, no re-read, `exit_out_of_tol` {bid 0.16, floor 0.17}, the rest at 0.18 — today's E4 path byte for byte. Book 285's shape: bids 0.70 / 0.71 / 0.82 send no IOC (the rest at 0.84), the bid at 0.83 sends the one IOC at 0.83 and nowhere under; the tolerance 0.01; `_exit_take` prices off `ex["take"]` / `ex["rest"]` alone. Book 661's shape: the replace's cancel books the 89.24 (ledger 1129), the re-plan sizes 520 off that ledger — never the row's 609 — the IOC fills 200, the 320 rest at 0.60; the cancel refused → nothing placed. At the unit (`_place` scripted): frozen / tripped / abandoned / a non-terminal row → one call, no rest, for a withheld and a partial IOC; every `IOC_SKIPPED` name rests the whole quantity; the remainder bounded by the plan (200 − 50 = 150) and by the ledger (100; a 120.5 row → 121; a 50.4 fill → 149); a whole fill or a ledger of 0 → `'take'`; the rest refused → `rested` 0, not counted; any other IOC result returned as is. Through the worker: E5's frozen long book's IOC (500, fills 100) leaves nothing resting; the short cover's partial IOC (S4) emits none of the new names; the call sites are the two in `_act` and no other function names `_exit_take`; `rules.exit_terms` on both sides as E4 built them; the census place, `_new_stats`, the emit site, the decision words, this section. RE-PINNED, each saying why: `test_mirror_live_worker.py`'s E4 pin "a partial IOC leaves nothing resting" (a rest of 200, the IOC fills 50) now reads the 150 resting at his cent the same tick, the next tick's cancel-and-take of the 150, then the bid gone → held; `test_e18_rest_life.py`'s "an exit IOC withheld when the bid fell" now reads the 200 resting the same tick with decision `exit_rest`; seven worker pins (thirteen cases with the increase-only guard's seven arms) whose fixture venue fills nothing on the exit IOC (`ioc_fill` 0) and read "one placement, the SELL" now read the IOC then the same-tick rest (the seven arms of the increase-only guards, the removed whale's book, the unreadable market read, exits-only mode, the venue-call guard at 0 with `ops` 1 → 2, the placement-429 abandon's next tick, `_exit_or_flip`'s SELL over a BUY rest); `test_e12_flow_only.py`'s sign flip on a flow book (the flip's IOC then the rest); `test_e12b_review_pins.py`'s F3 (the hold's cap reads the same-tick rest of 15 standing on the next tick, nothing placed); the E17 and E19 census-neighbour pins; and, found by the fold's full-suite run, five loss-stop pins (fourteen cases) that read "the reduce goes out" as one placement under a standing `mirror_loss_stop` or the sleeve's breaker — `test_l1_rearm_window.py` (2), `test_l1_review_pins.py` (1, five arms), `test_l2_review_pins.py`'s `_reduce_went_out` (6 callers), `test_l2_sleeve_window.py` (1) — now read the IOC then its same-tick rest: the rest is a reduce, and the day stop is read by no lane (`_loss_stop` byte-identical).

LIVE PROOF AFTER DEPLOY. A `book=<id>` read of the first row whose plan carries `exit_take_rested` beside `bid_moved` or a `take_filled` under `take_qty`; the hourly census reading `exit_take_rested` > 0 on a day with partial exit IOCs; lane 0b's `exits-band` section (plan lane 1: every exit IOC of the last 24 h on a long book — n, filled whole / partial / zero — and for each the `exit_rest` row on the same book placed within 15 s after it; before this lane that count is 0 by construction), beside exits-paired's `our_exit_filled_pct` as today.

DOES NOT FIX. The −2,190.22 paired bucket (FILL_plan section 0 item 2: pre-E15/E16 books, a short's covers, allocation); the frozen books' exits (266 and 601 on the paired set: our_settled − our_staked × his_roi_net = −977.65 − 977.65 × −0.849 = −147.63 and −251.94 − 292.32 × −0.580 = −82.39, hourly_1737 rows 1424 / 1436; 271 −365.36 and 347 −39.45 stuck, post_exits rows 332 / 338); a reduction he makes through the other token (223 / 269 / 310); the rest's own nature (post-only at ceil(his), no TTL: the shadow's 37.2% of his exit dollars unfilled, probe_1735_full.log 4139); the second cent of the exit take (owner decision D2, lane 3, inert); the short cover's partial IOC, which still rests nothing until the next tick (S4's path, untouched by the mandate of this lane); the wake on his next reducing fill on a book whose same-tick rest stands (`_fast_gate` `order_open`, as for every standing rest — before this lane the bare book after a partial IOC was fast-ticked): the full tick's keep branch takes it within `POLL_S`; lane 3 counts it `order_open_his_exit`. Named, not folded: the same-tick rest is sized on the IOC create's synchronous executions (`take_filled`) and the tick-start standing row (`_held`) — the contract `_entry_take` already trusts; a venue reporting more than was booked freezes the book `write_failed` with the row `unknown`, which the guards refuse — where today's next-tick rest reads a fresh row.
## 47. E14 (2026-09-08, FILL lane 2)

Owner (~17:45Z, the FILL program, verbatim): "We are being filled on his losers and missing his winners. That is adverse selection at the fill, not a sizing bug, and none of today's seven lanes addresses it directly. E14 (take at once when the ask is inside the band) is the lane for it and it is still pending". Decision D1 (a): the one-cent band live from the deploy, with the 24 h gate below.

THE RULE. On a LONG book, on a tick where NO order of ours stands (the open, the tick after a re-quote cancel, the fast tick's wake), an entry whose ask is ABOVE his cent but no more than `rules.MIRROR_TAKE_BAND` (0.01 of contract price) over his UNROUNDED price sends ONE IOC limited at `rules.band_cent(his)` = `buy_wire(his + band)` — floor-to-cent of his price plus the band, so no fill is ever more than the band over what he paid (his 0.471 → 0.48, his 0.479 → 0.48) — re-read at the send as every IOC is (E18, section 36), the unfilled remainder resting at his cent exactly as today. An ask at or under his cent takes as today (decision `'take'`, the at-level take fires FIRST and unchanged); above the band cent the rest as today (`'rest'`). A rest already standing is NEVER converted by this lane (the keep branch is E18's queue position — the gated lane 6); a short book's add is never band-taken; a reduce never reads the band. First sight only because the band table measures the ask AT PLACEMENT, so the exactly-1c tranche is exactly the population converted and nothing else. The rows (hard2/hourly_1737.txt 1746-1747, the 24 h entry-order band table): 1,922 entry rests, 1,827 with a band, 661 filled (34.4%), 16.0% placed at or through his cent, 42.2% inside 1c, 60.4% inside 2c, band median 2.0c, fill rate 42.2% inside 1c against 31.5% outside; 557 IOC takes, 364 filled (65.4%). The tranche this lane reaches: (42.2 − 16.0)% × 1,827 = 479 placements. The shapes it is built on: book 544 aec-wta-ipeoz-rosden (verify_1750 row 564: BUY 235 / 2,400 / 74 @0.520, every one at his 0.520) and book 611 cs2-g2-ast10 whose 16 IOCs expired 0 (hourly_1737 1726, `missed_expired_ioc` 16 / $4,929.95); Martinez 534 (hourly_1737 1172, task 71: his 0.61, the market 0.81 / 0.82); book 347's SHORT (book_347_1750 398, ORDER_INTENT_BUY_SHORT, `venue_ledger_disagree`); book 661's standing rest partially filled then replaced (closerows_1750 529, order 3632 — a SHORT book's `flatten_paired` cover rest, 609 @0.620 filled 89.24 then cancelled `replace`; the long-entry rest at 0.62 is the plan's stand-in for "a rest standing, the ask a cent above"). The honest expectation on today's rows is about zero to negative (FILL_plan lane 2, from FILL_design_fill 1.8: −73 to −112, −1,649 if the inside-band rests carry the filled class's −17.31%; judge 1's reading +99 / −2,376): the winners the owner named sit in fills no take reaches (below). The lane's case is the owner's order, brief question 3's read — for the first time every order row says whether it rested or took, and the take-band preset grades each word — and a bounded experiment the environment ends at zero cost.

THE CODE. `rules.MIRROR_TAKE_BAND = capped_env("MIRROR_TAKE_BAND", 0.01, floor=0.0)` beside `MIRROR_EXIT_TOL`; `rules.band_cent(his_px, band=None)` beside `buy_wire` (None when his price is not in (0, 1) or a bool, the band unreadable / negative / zero, the sum has no cent on the ladder, or the cent is not strictly above `buy_wire(his)` — a band under a cent is no band, at 0 the lane is off; `band` None reads the constant at call time); `rules.take_in_band(bid, ask, his_cent, band_cent)` beside `at_or_through` (`not at_or_through(BUY, his_cent) and at_or_through(BUY, band_cent)` — strictly above his cent, at or under the band cent, on a readable ask only, both cents readable with the band cent above his); `rules.order_decision(action, is_take, short_cover, in_band=False)` returns `'take_in_band'` only for an add's IOC with `in_band` True (a cover stays `'cover'`, a reduce's IOC `'take'`). `mirror_live._take_band(t, book, r, his_px, plan)` makes the read on every first-sight entry plan of a long book (a BUY add, not short) and stamps `plan["take_band"]`; `_act`'s no-order path reads it AFTER the arm's clearing and the at-level take and BEFORE the rest: `qty = _room_qty(t, p.qty, band, intent)` (the collateral at the band cent; `over_room` under a share), census `take_in_band`, `_entry_take(..., band, wire, qty, ..., first=True, in_band=True)` — the IOC through `_place` / `_place_reserved` with `in_band` threaded like `take_first` (the E18 re-read at the band cent, the room re-read, the 059 INSERT with `decision = rules.order_decision(action, is_take, cover, in_band=in_band and is_take)`), the IOC_SKIPPED rest and the remainder rest at `rest_px` = the wire = `buy_price(his, bid)` = his cent, decision `'rest'`, unchanged — sized from the PLAN's quantity by `_place_reserved`'s own room re-read at his cent (the review's LOW-1: the band IOC is sized on the room at the band cent, so a rest sized from the IOC's quantity would be `1 − his_cent / band_cent` smaller than today's at a binding cap — 1.9% at 0.52; handing the rests `p.qty` — the remainder `p.qty − take_filled` — and letting the re-read clip them at his cent makes the rest today's less the IOC's fill, never more). The row's `wire` is the band cent, `his_level` his price, `ask_at_send` the re-read's ask: the cents paid over him read per row as `wire − floor(his_level × 100) / 100`. Not touched (pinned by source and by hash against ab2525c): the keep branch's take off a standing rest, `_short_wire` and the short add's path, `rules.admission` and `MIRROR_CATCHUP_TOL_CENTS` / `_PCT` / `_MAX_CENTS` (the open's catch-up allowance is not a take band and the band is not an admission allowance), `_fast_gate` (a woken book with an order open is still `order_open`), every exit path, `_ioc_reread`, `exit_terms`, `rest_decision`, `take_allowed`, `at_or_through`, `buy_price`, the day stop. No migration: `'take_in_band'` is the word migration 059 reserved (its comment stands as written; the list it names, `'rest', 'take', 'cover', 'exit_rest'`, is now `'rest', 'take', 'take_in_band', 'cover', 'exit_rest'` at the INSERT, with the replace causes after a cancel as before).

THE NAMES. Census `take_in_band` — the band decision, counted where it is made beside `take_at_his_level`, whether or not the re-read lets the IOC out (the re-read's own names `ask_moved` / `ioc_reread_capped` / `ioc_quote_unread` say when it was withheld, `rest_placed` the rest that follows); inserted before E19's `drift_smaller_open` (keys[−13]) and `registered_no_increase` (keys[−12]) — keys[−14], after E20's `wrong_sign_hold` and E14b's `exit_take_rested` (sections 44 and 46), which landed first; the tail pins that count from the end move by one (−91 → −92, −92 → −93; E17's eight −23:−15 → −24:−16). Plan `take_band` {his_cent, band, band_cent, bid, ask, verdict} on every first-sight entry plan of a long book, verdict one of `at_level` (the ask at or under his cent: today's rule governs), `in_band` (the band IOC sent, or withheld by the re-read whose own field sits beside it), `out` (the ask above the band cent: the rest), `unread` (his cent unreadable, or the tick's ask missing or off the ladder: the rest), `off` (band cent None: the constant at 0 or under a cent), `uncounted` (the 059 columns absent this tick: the rest), and — a word the plan did not list, added because the plan is silent on it and a mislabel would be worse — `waiting` (the ask inside the band but `rules.take_allowed` not yet under a LENGTHENED `MIRROR_TAKE_AFTER_S`; at the default wait of 0 never written). `plan["decision"]` reads `'take_in_band'` as `'take'` is written today. Row `decision` `'take_in_band'` on the IOC, `'rest'` on the remainder or the withheld quantity; on a later replace cancel the cause words as today.

THE RAILS. `MIRROR_TAKE_BAND` 0.01 contract price, `capped_env` floor 0.0 — the environment may only LOWER it (0.02 lands on 0.01; 0.005 is honoured and is no band, since no cent lies inside it; 0 = the lane off, today's behaviour byte for byte; a negative value lands on the floor 0.0; junk / inf the default): the default IS the ceiling, so any wider band is a code change with a review. Not 2c and not the brief's max(2c, 10% of his cent) capped 5c: FILL_R5's table turns negative at 4c (−276) and 5c (−440). `MIRROR_TAKE_AFTER_S` 0.0 unchanged (`min_wait_env`); the band reads it through the same `rules.take_allowed` call the at-level take makes, so a lengthened wait makes the band rest-first too — and since the arm is cleared on a book not at his level and the keep branch never converts, under a lengthened wait the band does not fire at all (pinned: `waiting`, the rest). The band IOC is one op and one re-read, never a cancel: the replace budget (`MIRROR_MAX_REPLACES_PER_HOUR` 12) is not spent by it, and a re-quote refused `replace_capped` sends no band IOC either. No knob raises anything; the worker reads the constant through the rules module alone.

FAIL CLOSED. His cent unreadable → `unread`, the rest at the wire as today. The tick's ask missing or off the ladder → `unread`, the rest (a one-sided book with no ask is refused `no_mark` before any plan, as today). The band constant at 0 / under a cent / unreadable → `off`, the rest. The 059 columns absent (`t.order_cols` not True) → `uncounted`, the rest by the 050 INSERT — an uncounted band take is not allowed; the at-level take under the same absence is today's. The re-read's ask above the band cent → `ask_moved` {ask_at_plan, ask_at_send, wire = the band cent}, the whole quantity rests at his cent, one op; the re-read unreadable / the call budget spent → `ioc_quote_unread` / `ioc_reread_capped`, the rest. The room short at the band cent → `over_room`, nothing placed this tick (the take-first's own rule). The venue refusing the IOC, the tick tripped or abandoned, the book frozen, a row non-terminal → nothing more this tick, as today's take. The IOC filling nothing → the whole quantity rests at his cent (`missed_expired_ioc` in fills-missed, as today). The band is judged on the tick's quote and again on the re-read; both must hold.

## 48. The exit take named (2026-09-08, FILL lane 3)

Owner decision D2 (FILL_plan section 2): "exits stay within 1c" — so this lane lands the exit take's BAND machinery and names INERT, at a code default equal to the tolerance, where no behaviour changes; the second cent, if the owner grants it after lane 0b's `exits-band` read (section 45), is then ONE reviewed constant change and not a new lane. Beside it the fast gate's count of the wake it cannot answer.

THE RULE. `rules.exit_terms(side, his_px, tol=None, band=None)` reads a band at call time like the tolerance (`rules.MIRROR_EXIT_TAKE_BAND` with none given) and the effective band is b = max(tol, band) when the band is ABOVE the tolerance's own code default (`rules.EXIT_BAND_INERT_AT`, 0.01) and b = tol otherwise, so a band at or under that figure is no band at all — and a tolerance the environment LOWERED tightens the take exactly as before the band existed (E4 review LOW-6, its pin in test_mirror_live_worker: the plan's bare max(tol, band) would have let the band's default stand over a tolerance lowered to 0, an environment change that did not lower; found by that pin, corrected here and named as a departure from the plan's formula): on a long book's SELL the dict gains `band_floor` = his price − b and `take_band` = the lowest cent at or above it (the same ceiled reading as E4's `take`, so every fill is inside b of him at any precision; never a cent above `take`); on a short book's cover `band_ceiling` = his price + b and `cover_band` = the highest cent at or under it (capped at 0.99; never under `cover`). `take` / `rest` / `cover` are E4's byte for byte whatever the band reads. In `_act` the band test — `_exit_band_at(side, r, ex)`: the bid at or through `take_band` on a SELL, the ask at or under `cover_band` on a BUY, and ONLY when that cent is strictly past the tolerance cent — is a second `elif` AFTER the tolerance test on both long-exit sites (the keep branch's take off a standing rest, the no-rest exit) and both cover sites; a long book's band IOC goes through lane 1's `_exit_take(..., in_band=True)` (section 46: its withheld or unfilled quantity rests at his cent the SAME tick, exactly as the tolerance IOC's does), a short book's cover band IOC through the cover's own IOC exactly as its tolerance IOC goes (S4's path: a partial rests nothing this tick — otherwise untouched). At the default (0.01 = the tolerance) `take_band == take` and `cover_band == cover`, the tolerance branch fires first on every tick the band would, the `elif` is False by construction, and no row is ever written with a band word. The rows this is read against (hard2/post_exits_1707.txt, the exits-paired preset at 17:07Z, row 396): 354 books closed in 24 h, he cashed out on 93, we exited with him on 34, partially on 23, placed an exit that never filled on 10, placed none on 10, stuck_usd 1,190.43, lag median 75 s / p90 1,043 s, cents_vs_his median 0.4c. The shapes it is built on: book 334 cfb-smu-flst spread, his exit 0.549, our 358, filled 0.50 at a lag of 279 s (row 335); book 419 itf-olivar-hashimo, his 0.180, our 152, filled 0.09 at a lag of 3 s (row 336); book 285 lal-elc-rso total, his 0.840 in the preset (size-weighted), our exit average 0.718, filled 0.05 (row 334); book 467 atp-serna-jianu, his 0.250, our 570, filled 0.43 (row 333); book 347 wta-pareja-stefani, SHORT, his exit 0.514, our 2,477 with the cover placed and unfilled (row 338; book_347_1750 398: ORDER_INTENT_BUY_SHORT, `venue_ledger_disagree`); book 661's flatten_paired BUY_LONG 609 @0.620 filled 89.24 then cancelled `replace` (closerows_1750 529, order 3632); book 611 aec-cs2-g2-ast, LONG live, an ADD leg (hourly_1737 865). What the second cent would be worth is NOT claimed here (FILL_plan lane 3 and judge 1 item 1.1): the eleven single-episode long `partial_exit` / `exit_placed_unfilled` rows (post_exits 335, 336, 337, 340, 341, 343, 349, 350, 351, 352, 353) hold 179 + 138 + 59 + 77 + 96 + 41 + 12 + 64 + 3 + 11 + 14 = 694 shares never exited, settled −340.80, worth 401.79 at his price less 1c — i.e. at TODAY's take cent, what the code already earns whenever the bid is there; the second cent's whole cost on them is 694 × 0.01 = 6.94, and its increment is the `exits-band` bucket-2 line, in no file yet.

THE CODE. rules: `MIRROR_EXIT_TAKE_BAND = capped_env("MIRROR_EXIT_TAKE_BAND", 0.01, floor=0.0)` after `MIRROR_EXIT_TOL` (the 23rd downward-only rail; in `__all__`) and `EXIT_BAND_INERT_AT = 0.01` beside it (the tolerance's default restated, not a knob); `exit_terms` as above (an unreadable, negative or bool band, or one at or under `EXIT_BAND_INERT_AT`, is the tolerance: inert; a band cent that would land above the take / under the cover falls back to the tolerance cent); `order_decision(action, is_take, short_cover, in_band=False)`: a reduce's IOC with `in_band` True is `'exit_take_in_band'`, a short cover's IOC with it `'cover_in_band'` (an add's stays lane 2's `'take_in_band'`); the band words are written on an IOC alone, on a readable leg action alone, for `in_band` exactly True — anything else is the word the row carried before. mirror_live: `_exit_terms` stamps `exit_take_band` / `exit_band_floor` (long) or `exit_cover_band` / `exit_band_ceiling` (short) beside E4's four cents; `_exit_band_at` (the test), `_exit_band_take` (the test behind its count guard: a band IOC is asked for only with the 059 columns present this tick, `t.order_cols` True — the review's HIGH-1) and `_exit_band_mark` (the count and the plan's `exit_band` {bid, ask, his, cents_off_his, at} on the tick a band IOC goes); `_exit_held` adds the band bound (`band_floor` / `band_ceiling`) to `exit_out_of_tol` beside the floor / the ceiling, so the held tick records both bounds it was read against (equal at the default); `_exit_take(..., in_band=False)` limits its IOC at `ex["take_band"]` under the flag and threads `in_band` to `_place` (lane 2's plumbing; the rest that follows is the same rest at his cent, decision `'exit_rest'`; the plan's `exit_take_rested.take` is the IOC's cent); `QUIET_EXIT_PLANS` gains the two band names (a band take is an exit plan, not noise). The fast gate: `_Tick.fast_open` (the open rows the fast tick read, by book), `_order_open_his_exit(t, book, fills)` (True only when the fills are a list, the book's open row this fast tick read is an ENTRY rest — its side's leg action 'add', not an IOC — the prior plan carries E15's `reduce_ref.at`, and `mi.reducing_on` over the fills clocked after it is positive; anything unreadable or raising is False), `_fast_gate(t, book, fills=None)` whose `order_open` clause returns `order_open_his_exit` under that reading and `order_open` otherwise; `_fast_book` re-reads a bare `order_open` once with the market's fills of his (`ms.his_fills`: one table read, no venue call; unreadable → `order_open`) and counts the name — the read made only behind `_fast_open_entry_rest(t, book)` (the open row this fast tick read is an ENTRY rest, the one shape the split can name; a reduce rest, a cover rest, an IOC row or a stale `open_order_id` costs no table read — the review's LOW-1). Never a cancel, never a placement: the full tick's `qty` clause replaces the rest within POLL_S (rules.rest_decision, "a FALL never waits") and a cancel here would discard queue position on adds still wanted (FILL_plan lane 3, judge 1 sec 3; judge 2's X2(a) is not built, its count is). mirror_shadow: `exit_leg` records `exit_take_band_px` / `exit_band_floor` beside `exit_take_px`; `_resolve_previous` adds ONE `/* judge-band */` UPDATE on the SELL side — the bid came up to the row's band cent inside the same life — writing `would_fill_band` / `touched_s_band` / `touch_px_band` into the detail, never the live-compared column and never the long-only rate P1 is gated on; `_SQL_EXIT_CENSUS` reads `would_fill_band` (the band's own verdict; a row the rest judge resolved with a band cent recorded inherits it — the rest cent touched is the band cent touched, the plan expired is both expired; a row with no band cent is unjudged for the band); `summarize_exit_rows` reports `rate_band` and `unfilled_usd_band` (a share, like `unfilled_usd_share`) beside `rate` / `unfilled_usd_share` under the SAME judged-market gate, with `resolved_band` / `fills_band` / `resolved_usd_band` / `unfilled_usd_band_usd` as the counts behind them — so probe_1735_full.log 4139's line (rate 0.6064, unfilled_usd_share 0.3723 on resolved_usd 94,281.23) keeps a comparable pair.

THE NAMES. Census `exit_take_in_band` (a long book's band IOC, counted at the decision as `take_in_band` is, whether or not the re-read lets it out), `cover_in_band` (a short book's), `order_open_his_exit` (the fast gate's split: a `fast_tick_skipped` reason and a count) — ONE block inserted immediately before E19's `drift_smaller_open` (keys[−13]) and `registered_no_increase` (keys[−12]), after E14's `take_in_band`: keys[−16:−13]; the tail pins that count from the end move by three (test_mirror_live_worker −92: → −95:, −93 → −96; test_e9_review_pins −92:−88 → −95:−91, −93 → −96; test_e17 −24:−16 → −27:−19; test_e20 −16/−15/−14 → −19/−18/−17; test_e14b −15/−14 → −18/−17; test_e14 −14 → −17 and its `take_in_band + 1` neighbour; test_e19's predecessor chain from `drift_smaller_open`). Plan `exit_take_band` / `exit_band_floor` (long), `exit_cover_band` / `exit_band_ceiling` (short) on every priced exit plan; `exit_band` {bid, ask, his, cents_off_his, at} on the tick a band IOC goes; `exit_out_of_tol` {bid, ask, floor | ceiling, band_floor | band_ceiling, at}. Row `decision` `'exit_take_in_band'` / `'cover_in_band'` on a band IOC alone — at the default never written. Migration 059's comment stands as written; the list it names at the INSERT now reads `'rest', 'take', 'take_in_band', 'cover', 'exit_rest'` plus, on a band IOC, `'exit_take_in_band'` and `'cover_in_band'` (with the replace causes `'replace_cent'`, `'replace_qty'`, `'replace_side'`, `'ttl'`, `'replace_unread'` written over a cancelled row as before). The shadow's `exit_leg` block: `rate_band`, `unfilled_usd_band`, `resolved_band`, `fills_band`, `resolved_usd_band`, `unfilled_usd_band_usd`.

THE RAILS. `MIRROR_EXIT_TAKE_BAND` 0.01 of contract price, `capped_env` floor 0.0: the environment may only LOWER it (0.05 and 0.02 land on 0.01; 0.005 and 0 are honoured and are inert; junk / inf the default; a negative value the floor 0.0), and at or under the tolerance's default it changes nothing whatever `MIRROR_EXIT_TOL` reads — the default IS the ceiling, so the second cent is a code change with a review; a granted band is turned off by its own rail. `MIRROR_EXIT_TOL` 0.01 untouched, and its lowering still tightens the exit (a tolerance of 0 with the band at its default: the take at his cent, nothing worse). No wait added (no `min_wait_env`); the band IOC is an exit's op, exempt from the ops budget and the replace budget as every exit's is. The worker reads the band through `rules.exit_terms` alone (no env read of its own). The E12 ratchet, the E15 witness, the E16 gates, the E18 re-read (`_ioc_reread` untouched: called with the band cent as its wire, so `bid_moved` fires when the bid fell under the BAND cent between the read and the send) and the short cover's S4 path (`_s4_refusal`, `_cover_qty`, `_flatten_send`) are byte-identical; the $5,000 day stop is read by no site of the lane.

FAIL CLOSED. No price of his (`exit_px_src: 'none'`) → no band, today's unpriced behaviour. No bid on a SELL / no ask on a BUY, or a quote off the ladder → `at_or_through` False → held by name, no IOC. The band cent missing, unreadable, or not strictly past the tolerance cent → the band test is False → the tolerance rule alone. The 059 columns absent this tick (`t.order_cols` not True) with the touch inside the band → no band IOC (an uncounted band take is not sent, FILL_plan section 4): `exit_band_uncounted` on the plan, held by name, the rest at his cent by the 050 INSERT; the tolerance take under the same absence as before. The re-read unread / the bid gone → `ioc_quote_unread` / `bid_moved`, no IOC, and (lane 1) the whole quantity rests at his cent the same tick. The band unreadable in the environment → the default; a default that cannot be read → the floor 0.0 → inert. The reduce unwitnessed (E15 `reduce_unwitnessed`) or the ratchet unwitnessed (`flow_hold`) → no plan reaches `_act`; a frozen book behind E16's gates with the walk unread → `unclocked`, nothing sent; a tripped or abandoned tick, a frozen book, a non-terminal row → the band IOC's result returned, no rest. An entry leg → `_exit_terms` None, no band. The fast gate: the fills unreadable, the open row not this tick's read or not an entry rest, no reference clock, no reducing fill after it, any raise → `order_open` exactly as before; the split never cancels and never places. The shadow: the bid unread → no band judge; a row with no band cent → unjudged for the band; below the judged-market floor the band's rate and share are None.
